\displaystyle\varphi_{1,1,{\mathsf{r}},k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0}):=k(1+2\mu_{0})\left(\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)_{\mathsf{r}},\quad\varphi_{1,1,{\mathsf{s}},k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0}):=k(1-2\mu_{0})\left(\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)_{\mathsf{s}}

and
\|\widetilde{\varphi}_{0,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\|_{L^{\infty}(I_{3}(y_{0}))}\lesssim k^{\frac{1}{2}}{\mathcal{S}}_{k,0}
uniformly for all
0<\varepsilon<\varepsilon_{*}
. Additionally,
\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}
enjoys the more precise bound

\displaystyle\|\partial_{y}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\|_{H_{k}^{1}(I_{3}^{c}(y_{0}))}\lesssim{\mathcal{S}}_{k,0},\quad\|\partial_{y}^{n}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\|_{L^{\infty}(I_{3}^{c}(y_{0}))}\lesssim k^{n-\frac{1}{2}}{\mathcal{S}}_{k,0},

for
n=0,1,2
, uniformly for all
y_{0}\in I_{S}\cup I_{W}
and all
0<\varepsilon\leq\varepsilon_{*}
.

Proof.

From (
12.3
) we have

\displaystyle\|\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\|_{X_{k}}\leq\|T_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})\|_{X_{k}}+\|R_{1,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\omega_{k}^{0}\|_{X_{k}}+\|R_{0,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\varrho_{k}^{0}\|_{X_{k}}

and the estimate swiftly follows from Proposition
6.9
, Lemma
6.5
, Lemma
6.1
and the
Z_{k}
bounds on
\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}
from Proposition
12.1
. The decomposition (
12.4
) and the bounds for its coefficients are immediate. Likewise,
H^{1}_{k}(I_{3}(y_{0}))
estimate on
\partial_{y}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}
is a consequence of (
12.1
) and the
X_{k}
estimates on
\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}
. We next address the pointwise estimates.

\diamond

Proof for
n=0
.

Let
y\in I_{3}^{c}(y_{0})
and assume first that
y\in I_{6}(y_{0})\cap I_{3}^{c}(y_{0})
, with
y>y_{0}
, say. For
y_{3}=y_{0}+\frac{3\beta}{k}
we have

\displaystyle\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})=\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y_{3},y_{0})+\int_{y_{3}}^{y}\partial_{y}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(s,y_{0})\mathrm{d}s,

with
|\partial_{y}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y_{3},y_{0})|\lesssim k^{\frac{1}{2}}{\mathcal{S}}_{k,0}
due to the local bounds and

\left|\int_{y_{3}}^{y}\partial_{y}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(s,y_{0})\mathrm{d}s\right|\lesssim|y-y_{3}|^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{y}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\|_{L^{2}(I_{3}^{c}(y_{0}))}\lesssim k^{-\frac{1}{2}}{\mathcal{S}}_{k,0}

as well. On the other hand, if
y\in I_{6}^{c}{(y_{0})}
, then
I_{3}(y)\cap I_{3}(y_{0})=\emptyset
, and thus we can use the Sobolev-type inequality of Lemma
2.3
to show that

\displaystyle|\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})|\leq\|\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(z,y_{0})\|_{L^{\infty}_{z}(I_{3}(y))}\lesssim k^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(z,y_{0})\|_{H_{k}^{1}(I_{3}(y))}\lesssim k^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(z,y_{0})\|_{H_{k}^{1}(I_{3}^{c}(y_{0}))}\lesssim k^{-\frac{1}{2}}{\mathcal{S}}_{k,0}

uniformly for all
y_{0}\in I_{S}\cup I_{W}
.

\diamond

Proof for
n=1
.
We proceed as before, now writing

\displaystyle\partial_{y}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})=\partial_{y}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y_{3},y_{0})+\int_{y_{3}}^{y}\partial_{y}^{2}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(s,y_{0})\mathrm{d}s,

for
y\in I_{6}(y_{0})\cap I_{3}^{c}(y_{0})
. We have
|\partial_{y}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y_{3},y_{0})|\lesssim k^{\frac{1}{2}}{\mathcal{S}}_{k,0}
and further using the Taylor-Goldstein equation (
12.1
), the fact that the integration takes place in an interval of size roughly
k^{-1}
and the bound
\|\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\|_{L^{2}(I_{3}^{c}(y_{0}))}\leq k^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\|_{X_{k}}
we obtain the desired estimate. Similarly, for
y\in I_{6}^{c}{(y_{0})}
,we use Sobolev-type inequality of Lemma
2.3
as before.

\diamond

Proof for
n=2
.
Here we just use (
12.1
) and the previous
L^{\infty}(I_{3}^{c}(y_{0}))
bounds for
\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}
.
∎

On the other hand, for
y_{0}\in I_{M}
we have

Proposition 12.3
.

Let
k\geq 1
,
y_{0}\in I_{M}
and
\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})
a solution to (
12.1
). Then,

\displaystyle\sup_{y_{0}\in I_{M}}\|\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(\cdot,y_{0})\|_{LZ_{k}}\lesssim k^{-\frac{1}{2}}{\mathcal{S}}_{k,0},

uniformly for all
0<\varepsilon<\varepsilon_{*}
.

Proof.

From (
12.3
), we use Proposition
10.2
together with Lemma
6.7
and Lemma
6.8
.
∎

As before, with the
LZ_{k}
bounds for
\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}
at hand we can improve them to
LX_{k}
estimates.

Proposition 12.4
.

Let
k\geq 1
,
y_{0}\in I_{M}
and
\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})
a solution to (
12.1
). Then,

\displaystyle\sup_{y_{0}\in I_{M}}\|\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(\cdot,y_{0})\|_{LX_{k}}\lesssim k^{-\frac{1}{2}}{\mathcal{S}}_{k,0},

uniformly for all
0<\varepsilon<\varepsilon_{*}
. Moreover, we have

\begin{split}\partial_{y}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}&=\varphi_{{\mathrm{L}},1,1,{\mathsf{r}},k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})\eta^{-\frac{1}{2}+\gamma_{0}}+\varphi_{{\mathrm{L}},1,1,{\mathrm{L}},k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})\eta^{-\frac{1}{2}-\gamma_{0}}\log(\eta){\mathcal{Q}}_{\gamma_{0}}(\eta)\\
&\quad+\varphi_{{\mathrm{L}},1,1,{\mathsf{s}},k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})\eta^{-\frac{1}{2}-\gamma_{0}}+\widetilde{\varphi}_{{\mathrm{L}},0,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})\end{split}

(12.5)

where

\displaystyle\varphi_{{\mathrm{L}},1,1,{\mathsf{r}},k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})

\displaystyle:=k(1+2\gamma_{0})\left(\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)_{\mathsf{r}}(y,y_{0}),

\displaystyle\varphi_{{\mathrm{L}},1,1,{\mathrm{L}},k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})

\displaystyle:=k(1-2\gamma_{0})\left(\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)_{\mathsf{s}}(y,y_{0}),

\displaystyle\varphi_{{\mathrm{L}},1,1,{\mathsf{s}},k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})

\displaystyle:=2k\left(\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)_{\mathsf{s}}(y,y_{0}),

with
\|\widetilde{\varphi}_{{\mathrm{L}},0,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\|_{L^{\infty}(I_{3}(y_{0}))}\lesssim k^{\frac{1}{2}}{\mathcal{S}}_{k,0}

Moreover,
\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}
enjoys the more precise bound

\displaystyle\|\partial_{y}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\|_{H_{k}^{1}(I_{3}^{c}(y_{0}))}\lesssim{\mathcal{S}}_{k,0},\quad\|\partial_{y}^{n}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\|_{L^{\infty}(I_{3}^{c}(y_{0}))}\lesssim k^{n-\frac{1}{2}}{\mathcal{S}}_{k,0},

for
n=0,1,2
, uniformly for all
y_{0}\in I_{M}
and all
0<\varepsilon\leq\varepsilon_{*}
.

12.1.
Sobolev regularity for the first derivative

To obtain Sobolev regularity for
\partial_{y_{0}}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}
, we note that

\displaystyle\partial_{y_{0}}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}=\left(\partial_{y}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}+\partial_{y_{0}}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)-\partial_{y}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}.

Let
\varphi_{1,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0}):=\left(\partial_{y}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}+\partial_{y_{0}}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)(y,y_{0})
. There holds

\begin{split}\text{TG}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\varphi_{1,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}&=\frac{v^{\prime\prime\prime}(y)}{v(y)-v(y_{0})\pm i\varepsilon}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}-\frac{{\mathcal{P}}^{\prime}(y)}{(v(y)-v(y_{0})\pm i\varepsilon)^{2}}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\\
&\quad+2{\mathcal{P}}(y)\frac{v^{\prime}(y)-v^{\prime}(y_{0})}{(v(y)-v(y_{0})\pm i\varepsilon)^{3}}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}-v^{\prime\prime}(y)\frac{v^{\prime}(y)-v^{\prime}(y_{0})}{(v(y)-v(y_{0})\pm i\varepsilon)^{2}}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\\
&\quad+\frac{\partial_{y}w_{k}^{0}(y)}{v(y)-v(y_{0})\pm i\varepsilon}-w_{k}^{0}(y)\frac{v^{\prime}(y)-v^{\prime}(y_{0})}{(v(y)-v(y_{0})\pm i\varepsilon)^{2}}+\partial_{y}q_{k}^{0}(y),\end{split}

(12.6)

with now

\displaystyle\varphi_{1,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(0,y_{0})=\partial_{y}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(0,y_{0}),\quad\varphi_{1,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(0,y_{0})=\partial_{y}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(2,y_{0}).

Therefore, we have

\varphi_{1,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})+T_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\varphi_{1,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})=\left(R_{0,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\text{TG}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\varphi_{1,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)(y,y_{0})+{\mathcal{B}}_{0,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0}).

(12.7)

where we define

{\mathcal{B}}_{0,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0}):=\partial_{z}{\mathcal{G}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0},z)\varphi_{1,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(z,y_{0})\Big|_{z=0}^{z=2}.

(12.8)

We next argue according to the stratification regime.

12.1.1.
Strong and weak stratifications

In order to use Proposition
10.1
on (
12.7
) we first obtain
X_{k}
estimates on
{\mathcal{B}}_{0,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}
.

Lemma 12.5
.

Let
k\geq 1
. Then,

\displaystyle\|{\mathcal{B}}_{0,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\|_{X_{k}}\lesssim k^{\frac{1}{2}}{\mathcal{S}}_{k,0}

uniformly in
0<\varepsilon<\varepsilon_{*}
and
y_{0}\in I_{S}\cup I_{W}
.

Proof.

Firstly, since
\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(z,y_{0})=0
for
z=0,2
for all
y_{0}\in(0,2)
, we note that
|\varphi_{1,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(z,y_{0})|=|\partial_{y}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(z,y_{0})|\lesssim k^{\frac{1}{2}}{\mathcal{S}}_{k,0}
for
z=0,2
due to Proposition
12.2
. We next argue for
\partial_{z}{\mathcal{G}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0},z)
for
z=0
. We have

\displaystyle\partial_{z}{\mathcal{G}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0},0)=\frac{\phi_{{\mathsf{u}},k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})}{{\mathcal{W}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y_{0})}\partial_{y}\phi_{{\mathsf{l}},k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(0,y_{0}).

where there further holds, see
[
NIST
]
,

\partial_{y}\phi_{{\mathsf{l}},k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(0,y_{0})=-4k\gamma_{0}

(12.9)

Then,

\displaystyle\|\partial_{z}{\mathcal{G}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0},0)\|_{X_{k}}\lesssim 1

follows from (
12.9
), Proposition
4.1
and Corollary
4.3
. The lemma is then proved.
∎

We are now in position to prove the main regularity properties of
\varphi_{1,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}
in
X_{k}
.

Proposition 12.6
.

Let
k\geq 1
. There holds

\displaystyle\|\varphi_{1,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\|_{X_{k}}\lesssim k^{-\frac{1}{2}}{\mathcal{S}}_{k,1}

uniformly for all
y_{0}\in I_{S}\cup I_{W_{*}}
and all
0<\varepsilon\leq\varepsilon_{*}
. Moreover, we have

\displaystyle\|\partial_{y}^{n}\varphi_{1,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\|_{L^{\infty}(I_{3}(y_{0}))}\lesssim k^{-\frac{1}{2}}{\mathcal{S}}_{k,n+1}

for
n=0,1
, uniformly for all
y_{0}\in I_{S}\cup I_{W}
and all
0<\varepsilon\leq\varepsilon_{*}
.

Proof.

We use the limiting absorption principle Proposition
10.1
on (
12.7
) which provides

\displaystyle\|\varphi_{1,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\|_{Z_{k}}\lesssim\|R_{0,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\text{TG}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\varphi_{1,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\|_{X_{k}}+\|{\mathcal{B}}_{0,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\|_{X_{k}}.

From (
12.6
) we see that

\displaystyle\|\text{TG}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\varphi_{1,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\|_{X_{k}}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|R_{1,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\left(v^{\prime\prime\prime}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)\right\|_{X_{k}}+\left\|R_{2,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\left({\mathcal{P}}^{\prime}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)\right\|_{X_{k}}

\displaystyle\quad+2\left\|R_{3,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\left({\mathcal{P}}h_{1}(\cdot,y_{0})\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)\right\|_{X_{k}}+\left\|R_{2,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\left(v^{\prime\prime}h_{1}(\cdot,y_{0})\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)\right\|_{X_{k}}

\displaystyle\quad+\left\|R_{1,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\partial_{y}w_{k}^{0}\right\|_{X_{k}}+\left\|R_{2,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\left(h_{1}(\cdot,y_{0})w_{k}^{0}\right)\right\|_{X_{K}}+\left\|R_{0,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\partial_{y}q_{k}^{0}\right\|_{X_{k}}

where we have defined
h_{1}(y,y_{0}):=v^{\prime}(y)-v^{\prime}(y_{0})
. Thanks to Proposition
12.2
, we have
\|\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\|_{X_{k}}\lesssim k^{-\frac{1}{2}}{\mathcal{S}}_{k,0}
. Furthermore, Corollary
6.4
gives

\displaystyle\left\|R_{1,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\left(v^{\prime\prime\prime}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)\right\|_{X_{k}}

\displaystyle\lesssim k^{-1}\|\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\|_{X_{k}}\lesssim k^{-\frac{3}{2}}{\mathcal{S}}_{k,0}.

Similarly, Corollary
E.2
shows that

\displaystyle\left\|R_{2,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\left({\mathcal{P}}^{\prime}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)\right\|_{X_{k}}\lesssim\|\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\|_{X_{k}}\lesssim k^{-\frac{1}{2}}{\mathcal{S}}_{k,0},

while Lemma
E.4
gives

\displaystyle\left\|R_{3,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\left({\mathcal{P}}h_{1}(\cdot,y_{0})\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)\right\|_{X_{k}}\lesssim\|\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\|_{X_{k}}\lesssim k^{-\frac{1}{2}}{\mathcal{S}}_{k,0}

and Lemma
6.6
provides

\displaystyle\left\|R_{2,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\left(v^{\prime\prime}h_{1}(\cdot,y_{0})\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)\right\|_{X_{k}}\lesssim k^{-1}\|\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\|_{X_{k}}\lesssim k^{-\frac{3}{2}}{\mathcal{S}}_{k,0}.

Finally, Lemma
6.5
, Lemma
E.3
and Lemma
6.1
yield

\displaystyle\left\|R_{1,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\partial_{y}w_{k}^{0}\right\|_{X_{k}}

\displaystyle+\left\|R_{2,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\left(h_{1}(\cdot,y_{0})w_{k}^{0}\right)\right\|_{X_{K}}+\left\|R_{0,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\partial_{y}q_{k}^{0}\right\|_{X_{k}}

\displaystyle\lesssim k^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{y}w_{k}^{0}\|_{H_{k}^{1}}+k^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|w_{k}^{0}\|_{H_{k}^{1}}+k^{-\frac{3}{2}}\|\partial_{y}q_{k}^{0}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim k^{-\frac{1}{2}}{\mathcal{S}}_{k,1}.

With this and Lemma
12.5
, the
Z_{k}
estimate is established. The
X_{k}
estimate is then obtained as in Proposition
12.2
. The proof of the pointwise estimate is the same as in Proposition
12.2
.
∎

We are now in position to provide a useful description of
\partial_{y_{0}}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}
.

Proposition 12.7
.

Let
k\geq 1
and
y_{0}\in I_{S}\cup I_{W}
, we have

\begin{split}\partial_{y_{0}}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}&=-\varphi_{1,1,{\mathsf{r}},k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})\eta^{-\frac{1}{2}+\gamma_{0}}-\varphi_{1,1,{\mathsf{s}},k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})\eta^{-\frac{1}{2}-\gamma_{0}}+\widetilde{\varphi}_{1,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})\end{split}

(12.10)

where
\varphi_{1,1,\sigma,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})
are given as in Proposition
12.2
, with further
\|\widetilde{\varphi}_{1,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\|_{L^{\infty}(I_{3}(y_{0}))}\lesssim k^{-\frac{1}{2}}{\mathcal{S}}_{k,1}
. Moreover,
\partial_{y_{0}}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}
enjoys the more precise bound

\displaystyle\|\partial_{y_{0}}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\|_{H_{k}^{1}(I_{3}^{c}(y_{0}))}\lesssim k^{-1}{\mathcal{S}}_{k,1},\quad\|\partial_{y_{0}}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\|_{L^{\infty}(I_{3}^{c}(y_{0}))}\lesssim k^{-\frac{1}{2}}{\mathcal{S}}_{k,1},

uniformly for all
y_{0}\in I_{S}\cup I_{W}
and all
0<\varepsilon\leq\varepsilon_{*}
.

Proof.

There holds
\partial_{y_{0}}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})=-\partial_{y}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})+\varphi_{1,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})
and

\displaystyle\partial_{y}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})

\displaystyle=\partial_{y}\left(\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)_{\mathsf{r}}(y,y_{0})\eta^{\frac{1}{2}+\gamma_{0}}+2k\left(\frac{1}{2}+\gamma_{0}\right)\left(\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)_{\mathsf{r}}(y,y_{0})\eta^{-\frac{1}{2}+\gamma_{0}}

\displaystyle\quad+\partial_{y}\left(\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)_{\mathsf{s}}(y,y_{0})\eta^{\frac{1}{2}-\gamma_{0}}+2k\left(\frac{1}{2}-\gamma_{0}\right)\left(\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)_{\mathsf{s}}(y,y_{0})\eta^{-\frac{1}{2}-\gamma_{0}}.

Hence, we define

\displaystyle\widetilde{\varphi}_{1,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}:=\varphi_{1,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}-\widetilde{\varphi}_{0,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}

Thanks to Proposition
12.2
and Proposition
12.6
, we obtain the
L^{\infty}(I_{3}(y_{0}))
estimate for
\widetilde{\varphi}_{1,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}
. Next, the
H_{k}^{1}(I_{3}^{c}(y_{0}))
and
L^{\infty}(I_{3}^{c}(y_{0}))
bounds on
\partial_{y_{0}}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}
also follow from Propositions
12.2
and
12.6
.
∎

12.1.2.
Mild stratifications

In what follows we study the regularity of
\varphi_{1,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}
when
y_{0}\in I_{M}
. Firstly, the same arguments of Lemma
12.5
, Proposition
5.1
and Corollary
5.3
now provides

Lemma 12.8
.

Let
k\geq 1
. Then,

\displaystyle\|{\mathcal{B}}_{0,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\|_{LX_{k}}\lesssim k^{\frac{1}{2}}{\mathcal{S}}_{k,0},

uniformly in
0<\varepsilon<\varepsilon_{*}
and
y_{0}\in I_{M}
.

We next record the
LX_{k}
regularity of
\varphi_{1,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}
.

Proposition 12.9
.

Let
k\geq 1
. There holds

\displaystyle\|\varphi_{1,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\|_{LX_{k}}\lesssim k^{-\frac{1}{2}}{\mathcal{S}}_{k,1}

uniformly for all
y_{0}\in I_{M}
and all
0<\varepsilon\leq\varepsilon_{*}
.

Proof.

We show only the
LZ_{k}
estimate, since once it is established it can be upgraded to
LX_{k}
bounds as in Proposition
12.4
. We use the limiting absorption principle Proposition
10.2
on (
12.7
) which gives

\displaystyle\|\varphi_{1,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\|_{LX_{k}}\lesssim\|R_{0,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\text{TG}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\varphi_{1,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\|_{LX_{k}}+\|{\mathcal{B}}_{0,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\|_{LX_{k}}.

From (
12.6
) we see that

\displaystyle\|R_{0,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\text{TG}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\varphi_{1,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\|_{LX_{k}}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|R_{1,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\left(v^{\prime\prime\prime}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)\right\|_{X_{k}}+\left\|R_{2,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\left({\mathcal{P}}^{\prime}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)\right\|_{X_{k}}

\displaystyle\quad+2\left\|R_{3,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\left({\mathcal{P}}h_{1}(\cdot,y_{0})\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)\right\|_{X_{k}}+\left\|R_{2,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\left(v^{\prime\prime}h_{1}(\cdot,y_{0})\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)\right\|_{X_{k}}

\displaystyle\quad+\left\|R_{1,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\partial_{y}w_{k}^{0}\right\|_{X_{k}}+\left\|R_{2,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\left(h_{1}(\cdot,y_{0})w_{k}^{0}\right)\right\|_{X_{K}}+\left\|R_{0,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\partial_{y}q_{k}^{0}\right\|_{X_{k}}

where we have defined
h_{1}(y,y_{0}):=v^{\prime}(y)-v^{\prime}(y_{0})
. Thanks to Proposition
12.4
, we have
\|\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\|_{X_{k}}\lesssim k^{-\frac{1}{2}}{\mathcal{S}}_{k,0}
. Furthermore, Lemma
6.8
gives

\displaystyle\left\|R_{1,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\left(v^{\prime\prime\prime}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)\right\|_{LX_{k}}

\displaystyle\lesssim k^{-1}\|\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\|_{LX_{k}}\lesssim k^{-\frac{3}{2}}{\mathcal{S}}_{k,0}.

Similarly, Corollary
F.2
shows that

\displaystyle\left\|R_{2,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\left({\mathcal{P}}^{\prime}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)\right\|_{LX_{k}}\lesssim\|\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\|_{LX_{k}}\lesssim k^{-\frac{1}{2}}{\mathcal{S}}_{k,0},

while Lemma
F.3
gives

\displaystyle\left\|R_{3,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\left({\mathcal{P}}h_{1}(\cdot,y_{0})\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)\right\|_{LX_{k}}\lesssim\|\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\|_{LX_{k}}\lesssim k^{-\frac{1}{2}}{\mathcal{S}}_{k,0}

and Lemma
6.8
provides

\displaystyle\left\|R_{2,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\left(v^{\prime\prime}h_{1}(\cdot,y_{0})\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)\right\|_{X_{k}}\lesssim k^{-1}\|\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\|_{LX_{k}}\lesssim k^{-\frac{3}{2}}{\mathcal{S}}_{k,0}.

Finally, Lemma
6.8
, Lemma
6.7
and Lemma
2.3
yield

\displaystyle\left\|R_{1,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\partial_{y}w_{k}^{0}\right\|_{LX_{k}}

\displaystyle+\left\|R_{2,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\left(h_{1}(\cdot,y_{0})w_{k}^{0}\right)\right\|_{LX_{K}}+\left\|R_{0,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\partial_{y}q_{k}^{0}\right\|_{LX_{k}}\lesssim k^{-\frac{1}{2}}{\mathcal{S}}_{k,1}.

With this and Lemma
12.5
the proof is finished.
∎

We next establish a working formula for
\partial_{y_{0}}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}
for
y_{0}\in I_{M}
.

Proposition 12.10
.

Let
k\geq 1
and
y_{0}\in I_{M}
, we have

\begin{split}\partial_{y_{0}}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}&=-\varphi_{{\mathrm{L}},1,1,{\mathsf{r}},k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})\eta^{-\frac{1}{2}+\gamma_{0}}-\varphi_{{\mathrm{L}},1,1,{\mathrm{L}},k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})\eta^{-\frac{1}{2}-\gamma_{0}}\log(\eta){\mathcal{Q}}_{1,\gamma_{0}}(\eta)\\
&\quad-\varphi_{{\mathrm{L}},1,1,{\mathsf{s}},k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})\eta^{-\frac{1}{2}-\gamma_{0}}+\widetilde{\varphi}_{{\mathrm{L}},1,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})\end{split}

(12.11)

where
\varphi_{{\mathrm{L}},1,1,\sigma,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})
are given as in Proposition
12.4
, for
\sigma\in\{{\mathsf{r}},{\mathsf{s}},{\mathrm{L}}\}
, and
\|\widetilde{\varphi}_{{\mathrm{L}},1,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\|_{L^{\infty}(I_{3}(y_{0}))}\lesssim k^{-\frac{1}{2}}{\mathcal{S}}_{k,1}
. Moreover,
\partial_{y_{0}}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}
enjoys the more precise bound

\displaystyle\|\partial_{y_{0}}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\|_{H_{k}^{1}(I_{3}^{c}(y_{0}))}\lesssim k^{-1}{\mathcal{S}}_{k,1},\quad\|\partial_{y_{0}}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\|_{L^{\infty}(I_{3}^{c}(y_{0}))}\lesssim k^{-\frac{1}{2}}{\mathcal{S}}_{k,1},

uniformly for all
y_{0}\in I_{M}
and all
0<\varepsilon\leq\varepsilon_{*}
.

Proof.

The decomposition (
12.11
) follows from Proposition
12.4
. The pointwise estimates are then a consequence of Propositions
12.4
and
12.9
while the proof for the
H_{k}^{1}(I_{3}^{c}(y_{0}))
and
L^{\infty}(I_{3}^{c}(y_{0}))
estimate is identical to that of Proposition
12.6
.
∎

12.2.
Sobolev regularity for the second derivative

Finally, to obtain the main regularity structures of
\partial^{2}_{y_{0}}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}
, we recall
\varphi_{1,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}=(\partial_{y}+\partial_{y_{0}})\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}
and we record the identity

\displaystyle\partial_{y_{0}}^{2}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}=\partial_{y}^{2}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}+\left(\partial_{y}+\partial_{y_{0}}\right)\varphi_{1,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}-2\partial_{y}\varphi_{1,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}.

Then, for
\varphi_{2,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}:=(\partial_{y}+\partial_{y_{0}})\varphi_{1,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}
we have

\displaystyle\partial_{y_{0}}^{2}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}

\displaystyle=k^{2}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}+\frac{v^{\prime\prime}(y)}{v(y)-v(y_{0})\pm i\varepsilon}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}-\frac{{\mathcal{P}}(y)}{(v(y)-v(y_{0})\pm i\varepsilon)^{2}}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}+\frac{w_{k}^{0}}{v(y)-v(y_{0})\pm i\varepsilon}+q_{k}^{0}

\displaystyle\quad+\varphi_{2,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}-2\partial_{y}\varphi_{1,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}

with

\begin{split}\textsc{TG}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\varphi_{2,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}&=2\frac{v^{\prime\prime\prime}(y)}{v(y)-v(y_{0})\pm i\varepsilon}\varphi_{1,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}-2v^{\prime\prime}(y)\frac{v^{\prime}(y)-v^{\prime}(y_{0})}{(v(y)-v(y_{0})\pm i\varepsilon)^{2}}\varphi_{1,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\\
&\quad+4{\mathcal{P}}(y)\frac{v^{\prime}(y)-v^{\prime}(y_{0})}{(v(y)-v(y_{0})\pm i\varepsilon)^{3}}\varphi_{1,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}-2\frac{{\mathcal{P}}^{\prime}(y)}{(v(y)-v(y_{0})\pm i\varepsilon)^{2}}\varphi_{1,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\\
&\quad+\frac{v^{(4)}(y)}{v(y)-v(y_{0})\pm i\varepsilon}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}-2v^{\prime\prime\prime}(y)\frac{v^{\prime}(y)-v^{\prime}(y_{0})}{(v(y)-v(y_{0})\pm i\varepsilon)^{2}}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\\
&\quad+2v^{\prime\prime}(y)\frac{(v^{\prime}(y)-v^{\prime}(y_{0}))^{2}}{(v(y)-v(y_{0})\pm i\varepsilon)^{3}}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}-v^{\prime\prime}(y)\frac{v^{\prime\prime}(y)-v^{\prime\prime}(y_{0})}{(v(y)-v(y_{0})\pm i\varepsilon)^{2}}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\\
&\quad+4{\mathcal{P}}^{\prime}(y)\frac{v^{\prime}(y)-v^{\prime}(y_{0})}{(v(y)-v(y_{0})\pm i\varepsilon)^{3}}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}-\frac{{\mathcal{P}}^{\prime\prime}(y)}{(v(y)-v(y_{0})\pm i\varepsilon)^{2}}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\\
&\quad+2{\mathcal{P}}(y)\frac{v^{\prime\prime}(y)-v^{\prime\prime}(y_{0})}{(v(y)-v(y_{0})\pm i\varepsilon)^{3}}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}-6{\mathcal{P}}(y)\frac{(v^{\prime}(y)-v^{\prime}(y_{0}))^{2}}{(v(y)-v(y_{0})\pm i\varepsilon)^{4}}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\\
&\quad+\frac{\partial_{y}^{2}w_{k}^{0}(y)}{v(y)-v(y_{0})\pm i\varepsilon}-2\partial_{y}w_{k}^{0}(y)\frac{v^{\prime}(y)-v^{\prime}(y_{0})}{(v(y)-v(y_{0})\pm i\varepsilon)^{2}}\\
&\quad+2w_{k}^{0}(y)\frac{(v^{\prime}(y)-v^{\prime}(y_{0}))^{2}}{(v(y)-v(y_{0})\pm i\varepsilon)^{3}}-w_{k}^{0}(y)\frac{v^{\prime\prime}(y)-v^{\prime\prime}(y_{0})}{(v(y)-v(y_{0})\pm i\varepsilon)^{2}}+\partial_{y}^{2}q_{k}^{0}(y)\\
&=\sum_{j=1}^{17}{\mathcal{F}}_{j,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})\end{split}

(12.12)

and

\varphi_{2,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})+T_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\varphi_{2,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})=\sum_{j=1}^{17}\left(R_{0,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}{\mathcal{F}}_{j,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)(y,y_{0})+{\mathcal{B}}_{2,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0}),

(12.13)

where we further have

{\mathcal{B}}_{2,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0}):=\partial_{z}{\mathcal{G}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0},z)\varphi_{2,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(z,y_{0})\Big|_{z=0}^{z=2}.

(12.14)

We first obtain a more amenable form for
\varphi_{2,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}
at
z=0,2
.

Lemma 12.11
.

Let
k\geq 1
,
0<\varepsilon<\varepsilon_{*}
and
y_{0}\in[0,2]
. There holds

\displaystyle\varphi_{2,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(z,y_{0})=2\partial_{y}\varphi_{1,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(z,y_{0})

for
z=0,2
.

Proof.

We observe that
\varphi_{2,k,\varepsilon}=\partial_{y}^{2}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}+2\partial_{y}\partial_{y_{0}}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}+\partial_{y_{0}}^{2}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}
. Moreover
\partial_{y}\partial_{y_{0}}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}=\partial_{y}\varphi_{1,k,\varepsilon}-\partial_{y}^{2}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}
. Consequently,

\displaystyle\varphi_{2,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}=-\partial_{y}^{2}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}+2\partial_{y}\varphi_{1,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}+\partial_{y_{0}}^{2}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}

and the Lemma follows from (
12.1
), the compact support of
\omega_{k}^{0}
and
\varrho_{k}^{0}
, and the fact that
\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(z,y_{0})=0
, for
z=0,2
, for all
y_{0}\in[0,2]
.
∎

12.2.1.
Strong and weak stratifications

To use Proposition
10.1
on (
12.12
), we first obtain
X_{k}
estimates on
{\mathcal{B}}_{2,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}
.

Lemma 12.12
.

Let
k\geq 1
. There holds

\displaystyle\|{\mathcal{B}}_{2,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\|_{X_{k}}\lesssim k^{\frac{1}{2}}{\mathcal{S}}_{k,1}

uniformly for all
y_{0}\in I_{S}\cup I_{W}
and all
0<\varepsilon\leq\varepsilon_{*}
.

Proof.

From Lemma
12.11
we see that once
\left|\partial_{y}\varphi_{1,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(z,y_{0})\right|\lesssim k^{\frac{1}{2}}{\mathcal{S}}_{k,1}
the conclusion follows as in the proof of Lemma
12.5
. Indeed, now
\varphi_{1,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}
solves (
12.6
) and
\|\varphi_{1,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\|_{X_{K}}\lesssim k^{-\frac{1}{2}}{\mathcal{S}}_{k,1}
so that one can argue as in the proof of Lemma
12.5
to show that
\left|\partial_{y}\varphi_{1,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(z,y_{0})\right|\lesssim k^{\frac{1}{2}}{\mathcal{S}}_{k,1}
, we omit the details.
∎

Proposition 12.13
.

Let
k\geq 1
. There holds

\displaystyle\|\varphi_{2,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\|_{X_{k}}\lesssim k^{-\frac{1}{2}}{\mathcal{S}}_{k,2}

uniformly for all
y_{0}\in I_{S}\cup I_{W}
and all
0<\varepsilon\leq\varepsilon_{*}
.

Proof.

We shall use Proposition
10.1
on (
12.13
) to obtain
Z_{k}
bounds and then upgrade to
X_{k}
estimates. We recall from Propositions
12.2
and
12.6
that

\displaystyle\|\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\|_{X_{k}}\lesssim k^{-\frac{1}{2}}{\mathcal{S}}_{k,0},\quad\|\varphi_{1,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\|_{X_{k}}\lesssim k^{-\frac{1}{2}}{\mathcal{S}}_{k,1}.

Let
F_{j,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})=\left(R_{0,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}{\mathcal{F}}_{j,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)(y,y_{0})
, for
j=1,...,17
. Corollary
6.4
shows

\displaystyle\|F_{1,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\|_{X_{k}}+\|F_{5,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\|_{X_{k}}\lesssim k^{-\frac{3}{2}}{\mathcal{S}}_{k,1}

while Lemma
6.6
gives

\displaystyle\|F_{2,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\|_{X_{k}}+\|F_{6,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\|_{X_{k}}+\|F_{8,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\|_{X_{k}}\lesssim k^{-\frac{3}{2}}{\mathcal{S}}_{k,1}.

Furthermore, Lemma
E.4
yields

\displaystyle\|F_{3,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\|_{X_{k}}+\|F_{9,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\|_{X_{k}}+\|F_{11,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\|_{X_{k}}\lesssim k^{-\frac{1}{2}}{\mathcal{S}}_{k,1}

whereas Corollary
E.2
provides

\displaystyle\|F_{4,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\|_{X_{k}}+\|F_{10,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\|_{X_{k}}\lesssim k^{-\frac{1}{2}}{\mathcal{S}}_{k,1}.

From Lemma
E.5
we obtain

\displaystyle\|F_{7,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\|_{X_{k}}\lesssim k^{-\frac{3}{2}}{\mathcal{S}}_{k,0}

and from Lemma
E.7
we deduce

\displaystyle\|F_{1,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\|_{X_{k}}\lesssim k^{-\frac{1}{2}}{\mathcal{S}}_{k,0}.

Finally, using Lemma
6.5
for
F_{13,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}
, Lemma
E.3
for
F_{14,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}
and
F_{16,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}
, Lemma
E.6
for
F_{15,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}
and Lemma
6.1
for
F_{17,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}
we reach

\displaystyle\sum_{j=13}^{17}\|F_{j,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\|_{X_{k}}\lesssim k^{-\frac{1}{2}}{\mathcal{S}}_{k,2}.

With this and Lemma
12.12
the result follows.
∎

We are now ready to present a working formula for
\partial_{y_{0}}^{2}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}
.

Proposition 12.14
.

Let
k\geq 1
and
y_{0}\in I_{S}\cup I_{W}
, we have

\begin{split}\partial_{y_{0}}^{2}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}&=\varphi_{2,1,{\mathsf{r}},k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})\eta^{-\frac{1}{2}+\gamma_{0}}+\varphi_{2,1,{\mathsf{s}},k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})\eta^{-\frac{1}{2}-\gamma_{0}}\\
&\quad+\varphi_{2,2,{\mathsf{r}},k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})\eta^{-\frac{3}{2}+\gamma_{0}}+\varphi_{2,2,{\mathsf{s}},k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})\eta^{-\frac{3}{2}-\gamma_{0}}\\
&\quad+\frac{w_{k}^{0}(y)}{v(y)-v(y_{0})\pm i\varepsilon}+q_{k}^{0}(y)+\widetilde{\varphi}_{2,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})\end{split}

(12.15)

where

\displaystyle\varphi_{2,1,{\mathsf{r}},k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})

\displaystyle:=\left(2kv^{\prime\prime}(y)B_{\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})\left(\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)_{\mathsf{r}}-2k(1+2\gamma_{0})\left(\varphi_{1,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)_{\mathsf{r}}\right),

\displaystyle\varphi_{2,1,{\mathsf{s}},k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})

\displaystyle:=\left(2kv^{\prime\prime}(y)B_{\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})\left(\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)_{\mathsf{s}}-2k(1-2\gamma_{0})\left(\varphi_{1,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)_{\mathsf{s}}\right),

\displaystyle\varphi_{2,2,\sigma,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})

\displaystyle:=-4k^{2}{\mathcal{P}}(y)(B_{\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0}))^{2}\left(\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)_{\sigma},

and
\|\widetilde{\varphi}_{2,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\|_{L^{\infty}(I_{3}(y_{0}))}\lesssim k^{-\frac{1}{2}}{\mathcal{S}}_{k,2}
. Moreover,
\partial_{y_{0}}^{2}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}
enjoy the more precise bound

\displaystyle\|\partial_{y_{0}}^{2}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\|_{H_{k}^{1}(I_{3}^{c})}\lesssim k^{-1}{\mathcal{S}}_{k,2},\quad\|\partial_{y_{0}}^{2}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\|_{L^{\infty}(I_{3}^{c})}\lesssim k^{-\frac{1}{2}}{\mathcal{S}}_{k,2},

uniformly for all
y_{0}\in I_{S}\cup I_{W}
and all
0<\varepsilon\leq\varepsilon_{*}
.

Proof.

The decomposition (
12.15
), its local estimates, the
{H_{k}^{1}(I_{3}^{c}(y_{0}))}
and
L^{\infty}(I_{3}^{c}(y_{0}))
estimates are a consequence of the identity
\partial_{y_{0}}^{2}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}=\partial_{y}^{2}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}+\varphi_{2,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}-2\partial_{y}\varphi_{1,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}
and Propositions
12.2
,
12.6
and
12.13
.
∎

Mild stratifications

12.12
, we now have

Lemma 12.15
.

. There holds

\displaystyle\|{\mathcal{B}}_{2,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\|_{LX_{k}}\lesssim k^{\frac{1}{2}}{\mathcal{S}}_{k,1}

uniformly for all

, Lemmas

,
6.8
,
12.8
,
12.15
,

,

,

Proposition 12.16
.

Let
k\geq 1
and

. There holds

uniformly for all

.

We finish the section presenting a formula for
\partial_{y_{0}}^{2}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}
when

.

Proposition 12.17
.

Let
k\geq 1
and
y_{0}\in I_{M}
, we have

\begin{split}\partial_{y_{0}}^{2}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}&=\varphi_{{\mathrm{L}},2,1,{\mathsf{r}},k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})\eta^{-\frac{1}{2}+\gamma_{0}}+\varphi_{{\mathrm{L}},2,1,{\mathrm{L}},k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})\eta^{-\frac{1}{2}-\gamma_{0}}\log(\eta){\mathcal{Q}}_{1,\gamma_{0}}(\eta)\\
&\quad+\varphi_{{\mathrm{L}},2,1,{\mathsf{s}},k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})\eta^{-\frac{1}{2}-\gamma_{0}}\\
&\quad+\varphi_{{\mathrm{L}},2,2,{\mathsf{r}},k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})\eta^{-\frac{3}{2}+\gamma_{0}}+\varphi_{{\mathrm{L}},2,2,{\mathrm{L}},k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})\eta^{-\frac{3}{2}-\gamma_{0}}\log(\eta){\mathcal{Q}}_{1,\gamma_{0}}(\eta)\\
&\quad+\frac{\omega_{k}^{0}(y)}{v(y)-v(y_{0})\pm i\varepsilon}+\varrho_{k}^{0}(y)+\widetilde{\varphi}_{{\mathrm{L}},2,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})\end{split}

(12.16)

where

\displaystyle\varphi_{{\mathrm{L}},2,1,{\mathsf{r}},k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})

\displaystyle:=\left(2kv^{\prime\prime}(y)B_{\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})\left(\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)_{\mathsf{r}}-2k(1+2\gamma_{0})\left(\varphi_{1,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)_{\mathsf{r}}\right),

\displaystyle\varphi_{{\mathrm{L}},2,1,{\mathrm{L}},k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})

\displaystyle:=\left(2kv^{\prime\prime}(y)B_{\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})\left(\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)_{\mathsf{s}}-2k(1-2\gamma_{0})\left(\varphi_{1,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)_{\mathsf{s}}\right),

\displaystyle\varphi_{{\mathrm{L}},2,1,{\mathsf{s}},k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})

\displaystyle:=-4k\left(\varphi_{1,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)_{\mathsf{s}},

\displaystyle\varphi_{{\mathrm{L}},2,2,\sigma,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})

\displaystyle:=-4k^{2}{\mathcal{P}}(y)(B_{\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0}))^{2}\left(\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)_{\sigma},

for
\sigma\in\{{\mathsf{r}},{\mathrm{L}}\}
and
\|\widetilde{\varphi}_{{\mathrm{L}},2,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\|_{L^{\infty}(I_{3}(y_{0}))}\lesssim k^{-\frac{1}{2}}{\mathcal{S}}_{k,2}
. Moreover,
\partial_{y_{0}}^{2}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}
enjoy the more precise bound

\displaystyle\|\partial_{y_{0}}^{2}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\|_{H_{k}^{1}(I_{3}^{c})}\lesssim k^{-1}{\mathcal{S}}_{k,2},\quad\|\partial_{y_{0}}^{2}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\|_{L^{\infty}(I_{3}^{c})}\lesssim k^{-\frac{1}{2}}{\mathcal{S}}_{k,2},

uniformly for all
y_{0}\in I_{M}
and all
0<\varepsilon\leq\varepsilon_{*}
.

Proof.

The decomposition (
12.15
) follows from
\partial_{y_{0}}^{2}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}=\partial_{y}^{2}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}+\varphi_{2,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}-2\partial_{y}\varphi_{1,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}
and Propositions
12.2
,
12.6
and
12.13
. Likewise, the
{H_{k}^{1}(I_{3}^{c})}
estimate is proven arguing as in the proof of Proposition
12.7
, we omit the details.
∎

12.3.
Sobolev regularity for mixed derivatives

The purpose of this section is to obtain working formulas for
\partial_{y,y_{0}}^{2}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}
. Since
\partial_{y_{0}}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}=\varphi_{1,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}-\partial_{y}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}
, with
\varphi_{1,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\in X_{k}
, we observe that

\displaystyle\partial_{y,y_{0}}^{2}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})=\partial_{y}\varphi_{1,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})-\partial_{y}^{2}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0}).

We again present the estimates according to the location of
y_{0}
.

12.3.1.
Strong and weak stratifications

Here we consider the case where
y_{0}\in I_{W}\cup I_{S}
. Then, Propositions
12.2
,
12.6
and (
12.1
) provide:

Proposition 12.18
.

Let
k\geq 1
and
y_{0}\in I_{S}\cup I_{W}
. Then,

\begin{split}\partial_{y,y_{0}}^{2}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}&=\varphi_{3,1,{\mathsf{r}},k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})\eta^{-\frac{1}{2}+\gamma_{0}}+\varphi_{3,1,{\mathsf{s}},k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})\eta^{-\frac{1}{2}-\gamma_{0}}\\
&\quad+\varphi_{3,2,{\mathsf{r}},k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})\eta^{-\frac{3}{2}+\gamma_{0}}+\varphi_{3,2,{\mathsf{s}},k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})\eta^{-\frac{3}{2}-\gamma_{0}}\\
&\quad-\frac{w_{k}^{0}(y)}{v(y)-v(y_{0})\pm i\varepsilon}-q_{k}^{0}(y)+\widetilde{\varphi}_{3,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})\end{split}

(12.17)

where

\displaystyle\varphi_{3,1,{\mathsf{r}},k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})

\displaystyle:=-\left(2kv^{\prime\prime}(y)B_{\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})\left(\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)_{\mathsf{r}}-k(1+2\gamma_{0})\left(\varphi_{1,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)_{\mathsf{r}}\right),

\displaystyle\varphi_{3,1,{\mathsf{s}},k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})

\displaystyle:=-\left(2kv^{\prime\prime}(y)B_{\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})\left(\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)_{\mathsf{s}}-k(1-2\gamma_{0})\left(\varphi_{1,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)_{\mathsf{s}}\right),

\displaystyle\varphi_{3,2,\sigma,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})

\displaystyle:=4k^{2}{\mathcal{P}}(y)(B_{\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0}))^{2}\left(\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)_{\sigma},

and
\|\widetilde{\varphi}_{3,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\|_{L^{\infty}(I_{3}(y_{0}))}\lesssim k^{-\frac{1}{2}}{\mathcal{S}}_{k,2}
. Moreover,
\partial_{y,y_{0}}^{2}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}
enjoy the more precise bound

\displaystyle\|\partial_{y,y_{0}}^{2}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\|_{L^{2}(I_{3}^{c})}\lesssim k^{-1}{\mathcal{S}}_{k,2},\quad\|\partial_{y,y_{0}}^{2}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\|_{L^{\infty}(I_{3}^{c})}\lesssim k^{-\frac{1}{2}}{\mathcal{S}}_{k,2},

uniformly for all
y_{0}\in I_{S}\cup I_{W}
and all
0<\varepsilon\leq\varepsilon_{*}
.

Mild stratifications

For
y_{0}\in I_{M}
we now turn to Propositions
12.4
and
12.9
to obtain the following:

Proposition 12.19
.

Let
k\geq 1
and
y_{0}\in I_{M}
. Then, we have

\begin{split}\partial_{y,y_{0}}^{2}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}&=\varphi_{{\mathrm{L}},3,1,{\mathsf{r}},k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})\eta^{-\frac{1}{2}+\gamma_{0}}+\varphi_{{\mathrm{L}},3,1,{\mathrm{L}},k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})\eta^{-\frac{1}{2}-\gamma_{0}}\log(\eta){\mathcal{Q}}_{1,\gamma_{0}}(\eta)\\
&\quad+\varphi_{{\mathrm{L}},3,1,{\mathsf{s}},k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})\eta^{-\frac{1}{2}-\gamma_{0}}\\
&\quad+\varphi_{{\mathrm{L}},3,2,{\mathsf{r}},k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})\eta^{-\frac{3}{2}+\gamma_{0}}+\varphi_{{\mathrm{L}},2,3,{\mathrm{L}},k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})\eta^{-\frac{3}{2}-\gamma_{0}}\log(\eta){\mathcal{Q}}_{1,\gamma_{0}}(\eta)\\
&\quad+\frac{w_{k}^{0}(y)}{v(y)-v(y_{0})\pm i\varepsilon}+q_{k}^{0}(y)+\widetilde{\varphi}_{{\mathrm{L}},3,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})\end{split}

(12.18)

where

\displaystyle\varphi_{{\mathrm{L}},3,1,{\mathsf{r}},k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})

\displaystyle:=-\left(2kv^{\prime\prime}(y)B_{\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})\left(\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)_{\mathsf{r}}-k(1+2\gamma_{0})\left(\varphi_{1,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)_{\mathsf{r}}\right),

\displaystyle\varphi_{{\mathrm{L}},3,1,{\mathrm{L}},k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})

\displaystyle:=-\left(2kv^{\prime\prime}(y)B_{\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})\left(\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)_{\mathsf{s}}-k(1-2\gamma_{0})\left(\varphi_{1,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)_{\mathsf{s}}\right),

\displaystyle\varphi_{{\mathrm{L}},3,1,{\mathsf{s}},k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})

\displaystyle:=2k\left(\varphi_{1,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)_{\mathsf{s}},

\displaystyle\varphi_{{\mathrm{L}},3,2,\sigma,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})

\displaystyle:=4k^{2}{\mathcal{P}}(y)(B_{\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0}))^{2}\left(\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)_{\sigma},

for
\sigma\in\{{\mathsf{r}},{\mathrm{L}}\}
and
\|\widetilde{\varphi}_{{\mathrm{L}},3,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\|_{L^{\infty}(I_{3}(y_{0}))}\lesssim k^{-\frac{1}{2}}{\mathcal{S}}_{k,2}
. Moreover,
\partial_{y,y_{0}}^{2}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}
enjoy the more precise bound

\displaystyle\|\partial_{y,y_{0}}^{2}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\|_{L^{2}(I_{3}^{c})}\lesssim k^{-1}{\mathcal{S}}_{k,2}.

uniformly for all
y_{0}\in I_{M}
and all
0<\varepsilon\leq\varepsilon_{*}
.

13.
Inviscid damping estimates

In this section we present the proof of time-decay estimates on Theorem
B
.

13.1.
Decay estimates for
\psi_{k}(t,y)

Recall that we can write

\displaystyle\psi_{k}(t,y)=\frac{1}{2\pi i}\lim_{\varepsilon\rightarrow 0}\int_{0}^{2}e^{-ikv(y_{0})t}\left(\psi_{k,\varepsilon}^{-}(y,y_{0})-\psi_{k,\varepsilon}^{+}(y,y_{0})\right)v^{\prime}(y_{0})\mathrm{d}y_{0}.

Thanks to Lemma
11.6
, Proposition
11.5
and the Dominated Convergence Theorem we have

\displaystyle\psi_{k}(t,y)=\frac{1}{2\pi i}\lim_{\varepsilon\rightarrow 0}\int_{\vartheta_{1}}^{\vartheta_{2}}e^{-ikv(y_{0})t}\left(\psi_{k,\varepsilon}^{-}(y,y_{0})-\psi_{k,\varepsilon}^{+}(y,y_{0})\right)v^{\prime}(y_{0})\mathrm{d}y_{0}

(13.1)

and integrating by parts we reach

\displaystyle\psi_{k}(t,y)=\frac{1}{ikt}\frac{1}{2\pi i}\lim_{\varepsilon\rightarrow 0}\int_{\vartheta_{1}}^{\vartheta_{2}}e^{-ikv(y_{0})t}\left(\partial_{y_{0}}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{-}(y,y_{0})-\partial_{y_{0}}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{+}(y,y_{0})\right)\mathrm{d}y_{0}

where we have used Lemma
11.6
to show that the boundary terms vanish in the limit as
\varepsilon\rightarrow 0
and
\partial_{y_{0}}\psi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}=\partial_{y_{0}}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}
. We first prove point-wise inviscid damping in the strongly stratified regime.

Proposition 13.1
.

Let
k\geq 1
. Then,

\displaystyle\|\psi_{k}(t,\cdot)\|_{L^{\infty}(I_{S})}\lesssim k^{-\frac{5}{2}}t^{-\frac{3}{2}}{\mathcal{S}}_{k,2}

for all
t\geq 1
.

Proof.

Let
\mathsf{m}=\min_{j,n,=1,2}|\varpi_{j}-\varpi_{j,n}|
,
\delta_{0}=\min\left(\frac{3\beta}{k},\frac{1}{2}\mathsf{m}\right)
and let
\delta\in(0,\frac{\delta_{0}}{2})
. Let
B_{\delta}=B_{\delta}(y)
denote tha ball of radius
\delta
centred at
y
and
B_{\delta}^{c}=(\vartheta_{1},\vartheta_{2})\setminus B_{\delta}
. We split

\displaystyle\psi_{k}(t,y)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{ikt}\frac{1}{2\pi i}\lim_{\varepsilon\rightarrow 0}\left(\int_{B_{\delta}}+\int_{B_{\delta}^{c}}\right)e^{-ikv(y_{0})t}\left(\partial_{y_{0}}\psi_{k,\varepsilon}^{-}(y,y_{0})-\partial_{y_{0}}\psi_{k,\varepsilon}^{+}(y,y_{0})\right)\mathrm{d}y_{0}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{ikt}\frac{1}{2\pi i}\lim_{\varepsilon\rightarrow 0}\left({\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,1}+{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,2}\right)

We begin with
{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,1}
. Since
|y-y_{0}|\leq\frac{\delta}{2}<\frac{3\beta}{k}
, we have that
y\in I_{3}(y_{0})
and
y_{0}\in I_{S}
so that
\gamma_{0}\in i{\mathbb{R}}
and we can use Proposition
12.7
to write

\displaystyle\left|\partial_{y_{0}}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})\right|\lesssim k^{-\frac{1}{2}}(k|y-y_{0}|)^{-\frac{1}{2}}{\mathcal{S}}_{k,1}

for all
y\in I_{S}
and all
y_{0}\in I_{3}(y)
. Hence,
|{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,1}|\lesssim k^{-\frac{3}{2}}(k\delta)^{\frac{1}{2}}{\mathcal{S}}_{k,1}
for all
y\in I_{S}
. For
{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,2}
we integrate by parts once more,

\displaystyle{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{ikt}\frac{e^{-ikv(y_{0})t}}{v^{\prime}(y_{0})}\left(\partial_{y_{0}}\psi_{k,\varepsilon}^{-}(y,y_{0})-\partial_{y_{0}}\psi_{k,\varepsilon}^{+}(y,y_{0})\right)\Big|_{y_{0}\in\partial B_{\delta}^{c}}

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{1}{ikt}\int_{B_{\delta}^{c}}\frac{e^{-ikv(y_{0})t}}{v^{\prime}(y_{0})}\left(\partial^{2}_{y_{0}}\psi_{k,\varepsilon}^{-}(y,y_{0})-\partial^{2}_{y_{0}}\psi_{k,\varepsilon}^{+}(y,y_{0})\right)\mathrm{d}y_{0}

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{1}{ikt}\int_{B_{\delta}^{c}}{e^{-ikv(y_{0})t}}\partial_{y_{0}}\left(\frac{1}{v^{\prime}(y_{0})}\right)\left(\partial_{y_{0}}\psi_{k,\varepsilon}^{-}(y,y_{0})-\partial_{y_{0}}\psi_{k,\varepsilon}^{+}(y,y_{0})\right)\mathrm{d}y_{0}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{ikt}\left(-{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,2,1}+{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,2,2}+{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,2,3}\right).

To estimate
{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,2,1}
we use the local bounds and the global pointwise bounds of Proposition
12.7
when
y_{0}\in\partial B_{\delta}\cap B_{\delta_{0}}
and
y_{0}\in\partial B_{\delta}^{c}\cap B_{\delta_{0}}^{c}
, respectively, to obtain

\displaystyle\left|{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,2,1}\right|\lesssim k^{-\frac{1}{2}}\left(1+(k\delta)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right){\mathcal{S}}_{k,1}.

For
{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,2,2}
we further split

\displaystyle{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,2,2}

\displaystyle=\left(\int_{B_{\delta}^{c}\cap B_{\delta_{0}}}+\int_{B_{\delta_{0}}^{c}}\right)\frac{e^{-ikv(y_{0})t}}{v^{\prime}(y_{0})}\left(\partial^{2}_{y_{0}}\psi_{k,\varepsilon}^{-}(y,y_{0})-\partial^{2}_{y_{0}}\psi_{k,\varepsilon}^{+}(y,y_{0})\right)\mathrm{d}y_{0}

\displaystyle={\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,2,2,1}+{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,2,2,2}.

Moreover, thanks to (
12.15
), we note that

\displaystyle\lim_{\varepsilon\rightarrow 0}\int_{B_{\delta}^{c}}\frac{e^{-ikv(y_{0})t}}{v^{\prime}(y_{0})}\left(\frac{w_{k}^{0}(y)}{v(y)-v(y_{0})-i\varepsilon}-\frac{w_{k}^{0}(y)}{v(y)-v(y_{0})+i\varepsilon}\right)\mathrm{d}y_{0}=0

due to the dominated convergence theorem because
|y-y_{0}|>\frac{\delta}{2}>0
. Next, for all
y_{0}\in B_{\delta}^{c}\cap B_{\delta_{0}}
we have
y_{0}\in I_{S}
and thus integrating the local decomposition and estimates of Proposition
12.14
yields

\displaystyle\left|\lim_{\varepsilon\rightarrow 0}{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,2,2,1}\right|\lesssim k^{-\frac{3}{2}}(k\delta)^{-\frac{1}{2}}{\mathcal{S}}_{k,2}.

On the other hand, the global pointwise bounds of Propositions
12.14
and
12.17
when
\delta_{0}=\frac{3\beta}{k}
and the local and global estimates of Propositions
12.14
and
12.17
when
\delta_{0}=\frac{1}{2}\mathsf{m}
give

\displaystyle\left|\lim_{\varepsilon\rightarrow 0}{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,2,2,2}\right|\lesssim k^{-\frac{1}{2}}{\mathcal{S}}_{k,2}.

Hence,
\left|\lim_{\varepsilon\rightarrow 0}{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,2,2}\right|\lesssim k^{-\frac{1}{2}}(k\delta)^{-\frac{1}{2}}{\mathcal{S}}_{k,2}
. Similarly, we also deduce that
\left|\lim_{\varepsilon\rightarrow 0}{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,2,3}\right|\lesssim k^{-\frac{1}{2}}(k\delta)^{-\frac{1}{2}}{\mathcal{S}}_{k,2}
. Therefore,

\displaystyle\left|\psi_{k}(t,y)\right|\lesssim(kt)^{-1}k^{-\frac{3}{2}}(k\delta)^{\frac{1}{2}}{\mathcal{S}}_{k,1}+(kt)^{-2}k^{-\frac{1}{2}}(k\delta)^{-\frac{1}{2}}{\mathcal{S}}_{k,2}.

Optimizing for
\delta
, we see that
\delta=\frac{1}{kt}\min\left(\beta,\frac{k\mathsf{m}}{4}\right)
gives

\displaystyle\left|\psi_{k}(t,y)\right|\lesssim k^{-\frac{5}{2}}t^{-\frac{3}{2}}{\mathcal{S}}_{k,2}

and the proof is concluded.
∎

We next address the bounds for
\psi_{k}(t,y)
when
y\in I_{M}
. Recall that
I_{M}=[\varpi_{1,1},\varpi_{1,2}]\cup[\varpi_{2,1},\varpi_{2,2}]\setminus\{\varpi_{1},\varpi_{2}\}
. We have

Proposition 13.2
.

Let
k\geq 1
and
t\geq 1
. Then,

\displaystyle\left|\psi_{k}(t,y)\right|\lesssim k^{-\frac{5}{2}}t^{-\frac{3}{2}+\mu(y)}(1+\log(t)){\mathcal{S}}_{k,2}

for
y\in[\varpi_{1,1},\varpi_{1,2}]\cup[\varpi_{2,1},\varpi_{2,2}]
.

Proof.

We shall prove the bounds for
y\in[\varpi_{2,1},\varpi_{2,2}]
. Define
\delta_{0}=\frac{3\beta}{k}
and let
\delta\in(0,\frac{\delta_{0}}{2})
. We write

\displaystyle\int_{\vartheta_{1}}^{\vartheta_{2}}e^{-ikv(y_{0})t}

\displaystyle\left(\partial_{y_{0}}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{-}(y,y_{0})-\partial_{y_{0}}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{+}(y,y_{0})\right)\mathrm{d}y_{0}

\displaystyle=\int_{B_{\delta}}e^{-ikv(y_{0})t}\left(\partial_{y_{0}}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{-}(y,y_{0})-\partial_{y_{0}}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{+}(y,y_{0})\right)\mathrm{d}y_{0}

\displaystyle\quad+\int_{B_{\delta}^{c}}e^{-ikv(y_{0})t}\left(\partial_{y_{0}}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{-}(y,y_{0})-\partial_{y_{0}}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{+}(y,y_{0})\right)\mathrm{d}y_{0}

\displaystyle={\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,1}+{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,2},

where we recall
B_{\delta}=B_{\delta}(y)
denotes the ball of radius
\delta>0
centred at
y
and
B_{\delta}^{c}=(\vartheta_{1},\vartheta_{2})\setminus B_{\delta}
.

\bullet

Estimates for
{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,1}
.
Since
\delta<\frac{3\beta}{k}
we have that
y\in I_{3}(y_{0})
, for all
y_{0}\in B_{\delta}
. Thus, Proposition
12.7
for
y_{0}\in B_{\delta}\cap(I_{S}\cup I_{W})
and Proposition
12.10
for
y_{0}\in B_{\delta}\cap I_{M}
yield

\displaystyle|{\mathcal{I}}_{1}|\lesssim k^{-\frac{1}{2}}{\mathcal{S}}_{k,1}\int_{B_{\delta}}(k|y-y_{0}|)^{-\frac{1}{2}+\mu_{0}}+(k|y-y_{0}|)^{-\frac{1}{2}-\mu_{0}}+(k|y-y_{0}|)^{-\frac{1}{2}-\mu_{0}}|\log(k|y-y_{0}|)|\mathrm{d}y_{0}.

Recall that
\mu_{0}>0
for
y_{0}\in(\varpi_{2},\varpi_{2,2}]
while it is identically zero for
y_{0}\in[\varpi_{2,1},\varpi_{2})
. Moreover,

\displaystyle(k|y_{0}-y|)^{-\mu_{0}}

\displaystyle=(k|y_{0}-y|)^{-\mu(y)}e^{(\mu(y)-\mu(y_{0}))\log(k|y_{0}-y|)}

\displaystyle\lesssim(k|y_{0}-y|)^{-\mu(y)}

because
e^{(\mu(y)-\mu(z))\log(k|y_{0}-y|)}\lesssim e^{k^{-1}(k|y-y_{0}|)\log(k|y-y_{0}|)}\lesssim 1
as
(k|y-y_{0}|)\log(k|y-y_{0}|)\lesssim 1
since
k|y-y_{0}|\lesssim 1
. Hence,

\displaystyle\left|{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,1}\right|\lesssim k^{-\frac{3}{2}}(k\delta)^{\frac{1}{2}-\mu(y)}\left(1-\log(k\delta)\right){\mathcal{S}}_{k,1},

where we have used that

\displaystyle\left|\int_{0}^{x}u^{-\frac{1}{2}-\mu}\log(u)\mathrm{d}u\right|=-\int_{0}^{x}u^{-\frac{1}{2}-\mu}\log(u)\mathrm{d}u

\displaystyle=-\int_{0}^{x}\partial_{u}\left(\frac{u^{\frac{1}{2}-\mu}}{\frac{1}{2}-\mu}\right)\log(u)\mathrm{d}u

\displaystyle\lesssim x^{\frac{1}{2}-\mu}\left(1+|\log(x)|\right),

for all
x<1
and all
\mu<\frac{1}{2}
. This is indeed the case for all
y\in I_{M}
.

\bullet

Estimates for
{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,2}
.
We integrate by parts again,

\displaystyle{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,2}

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{ikt}\frac{e^{-ikv(y_{0})t}}{v^{\prime}(y_{0})}\left(\partial_{y_{0}}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{-}(y,y_{0})-\partial_{y_{0}}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{+}(y,y_{0})\right)\Big|_{y_{0}\in\partial B^{c}_{\delta}}

(13.2)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{ikt}\int_{B_{\delta}^{c}}\frac{e^{-ikv(y_{0})t}}{v^{\prime}(y_{0})}\left(\partial_{y_{0}}^{2}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{-}(y,y_{0})-\partial_{y_{0}}^{2}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{+}(y,y_{0})\right)\mathrm{d}y_{0}

(13.3)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{ikt}\int_{B_{\delta}^{c}}e^{-ikv(y_{0})t}\partial_{y_{0}}\left(\frac{1}{v^{\prime}(y_{0})}\right)\left(\partial_{y_{0}}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{-}(y,y_{0})-\partial_{y_{0}}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{+}(y,y_{0})\right)\mathrm{d}y_{0}

(13.4)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{ikt}\left(-{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,2,1}+{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,2,2}+{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,2,3}\right).

(13.5)

We treat each term separately.

\diamond

Estimates for
{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,2,1}
.
For
y_{0}\in B_{\delta_{0}}
we use Proposition
12.7
if
y_{0}\in(I_{S}\cup I_{W})
and Proposition
12.10
if
y_{0}\in I_{M}
, while for
y_{0}\in B_{\delta_{0}}^{c}
we use the pointwise global estimates of Propositions
12.7
and
12.10
to deduce that

\displaystyle|{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,2,1}|\lesssim k^{-\frac{1}{2}}(k\delta)^{-\frac{1}{2}-\mu(y)}(1-\log(k\delta)){\mathcal{S}}_{k,1}.

\diamond

Estimates for
{\mathcal{I}}_{2,2}
.
We subdivide again

\displaystyle{\mathcal{I}}_{2,2}

\displaystyle=\left(\int_{B_{\delta}^{c}\cap I_{3}(y)}+\int_{B_{\delta}^{c}\cap I_{3}^{c}(y)}\right)\frac{e^{-ikv(y_{0})t}}{v^{\prime}(y_{0})}\left(\partial_{y_{0}}^{2}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{-}(y,y_{0})-\partial_{y_{0}}^{2}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{+}(y,y_{0})\right)\mathrm{d}y_{0}

\displaystyle={\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,2,2,1}+{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,2,2,2}.

Arguing as in
{\mathcal{I}}_{2,2,2}
above, from Propositions
12.14
and
12.17
we deduce that integrating the local decomposition and bounds give

\displaystyle|\lim_{\varepsilon\rightarrow 0}{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,2,2,1}|\lesssim k^{-\frac{3}{2}}(k\delta)^{-\frac{1}{2}-\mu(y)}(1-\log(k\delta)){\mathcal{S}}_{k,2}

while the global pointwise estimates provide

\displaystyle|\lim_{\varepsilon\rightarrow 0}{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,2,2,2}|\lesssim k^{-\frac{1}{2}}{\mathcal{S}}_{k,2}.

Hence, we have
|\lim_{\varepsilon\rightarrow 0}{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,2,2,2}|\lesssim k^{-\frac{1}{2}}(k\delta)^{-\frac{1}{2}-\mu(y)}(1-\log(k\delta)){\mathcal{S}}_{k,2}
.

\diamond

Estimates for
{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,2,3}
.
Proceeding as in
{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,2,2}
, we see from Propositions
12.7
and
12.10
that

\displaystyle|\lim_{\varepsilon\rightarrow 0}{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,2,3}|\lesssim k^{-\frac{1}{2}}(k\delta)^{-\frac{1}{2}-\mu(y)}(1-\log(k\delta)){\mathcal{S}}_{k,2}

as well.

\bullet

End of proof.

Gathering all the estimates, we read

\displaystyle\left|\psi_{k}(t,y)\right|\lesssim k^{-\frac{3}{2}}(kt)^{-1}(k\delta)^{\frac{1}{2}-\mu(y)}(1-\log(k\delta)){\mathcal{S}}_{k,2}+k^{-\frac{1}{2}}(kt)^{-2}(k\delta)^{-\frac{1}{2}-\mu(y)}(1-\log(k\delta)){\mathcal{S}}_{k,2}

which yields the desired estimate for
\delta=\frac{\beta}{kt}
.
∎

We next show the decay rates in the weak regime.

Proposition 13.3
.

Let
k\geq 1
and
t\geq 1
. Then,

\displaystyle\left|\psi_{k}(t,y)\right|\lesssim k^{-\frac{5}{2}}t^{-\frac{3}{2}+\mu(y)}{\mathcal{S}}_{k,2}

for all
y\in(\vartheta_{1},\varpi_{1,1})\cup(\varpi_{2,2},\vartheta_{2})
.

Proof.

We show the estimates for
y\in(\varpi_{2,2},\vartheta_{2})
. Proceeding as before, let
\delta_{0}=\min\left(\frac{3\beta}{k},\frac{\varpi_{2,2}-\varpi_{2}}{2}\right)
, let
\delta\in(0,\frac{\delta_{0}}{2})
and we consider

\displaystyle{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,1}=\int_{B_{\delta}(y)}e^{-ikv(y_{0})y}\left(\partial_{y_{0}}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{-}(y,y_{0})-\partial_{y_{0}}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{+}(y,y_{0})\right)\mathrm{d}y_{0}

and

\displaystyle{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,2}=\int_{B_{\delta}^{c}(y)}e^{-ikv(y_{0})y}\left(\partial_{y_{0}}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{-}(y,y_{0})-\partial_{y_{0}}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{+}(y,y_{0})\right)\mathrm{d}y_{0}

as before, so that

\displaystyle\psi_{k}(t,y)=\frac{1}{ikt}\frac{1}{2\pi i}\lim_{\varepsilon\rightarrow 0}\left({\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,1}+{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,2}\right).

\bullet

Estimates for
{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,1}
.
As usual, since
\delta<\frac{3\beta}{k}
we have
y\in I_{3}(y_{0})
and
y_{0}\in I_{W}
for all
y_{0}\in B_{\delta}
. Then, Proposition
12.7
gives

\displaystyle|{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,1}|

\displaystyle\lesssim k^{-\frac{1}{2}}{\mathcal{S}}_{k,1}\int_{y-\delta}^{y+\delta}\left((k|y-y_{0}|)^{-\frac{1}{2}+\mu_{0}}+(1-2\mu_{0})(k|y-y_{0}|)^{-\frac{1}{2}-\mu_{0}}\right)\mathrm{d}y_{0}

\displaystyle\lesssim k^{-\frac{1}{2}}{\mathcal{S}}_{k,1}\int_{y-\delta}^{y+\delta}\left(k^{-1}+(k|y-y_{0}|)^{-\frac{1}{2}+\mu(y)}+(1-2\mu(y))(k|y-y_{0}|)^{\frac{1}{2}-\mu(y)}\right)\mathrm{d}y_{0}

\displaystyle\lesssim k^{-\frac{3}{2}}(k\delta)^{\frac{1}{2}-\mu(y)}{\mathcal{S}}_{k,1},

where we have used that
|\mu_{0}-\mu(y)|\lesssim|y-y_{0}|
and that
(k|y-y_{0}|)^{-\mu_{0}}\lesssim(k|y-y_{0}|)^{-\mu(y)}
because
k|y-y_{0}|\leq 1
.

\bullet

Estimates for
{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,2}
.
We further integrate by parts in
{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,2}
and split into
{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,2,1}
,
{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,2,2}
and
{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,2,3}
as in (
13.2
) - (
13.4
).

\diamond

Estimates for
{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,2,1}
.
For

\displaystyle{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,2,1}=\frac{e^{-ikv(y_{0})t}}{v^{\prime}(y_{0})}\left(\partial_{y_{0}}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{-}(y,y_{0})-\partial_{y_{0}}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{+}(y,y_{0})\right)\Big|_{y_{0}\in\partial B_{\delta}^{c}}

we note that for
|y-y_{0}|=\delta
we can use the local estimates of Proposition
12.7
, while for
y_{0}\rightarrow\vartheta_{2}
or
y_{0}\rightarrow\vartheta_{1}
we can use either the local or global bounds of Proposition
12.7
to deduce that

\displaystyle|{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,2,1}|\lesssim k^{-\frac{1}{2}}(k\delta)^{-\frac{1}{2}-\mu(y)}{\mathcal{S}}_{k,1}

\diamond

Estimates for
{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,2,2}
.
Next,

\displaystyle{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,2,2}

\displaystyle=\int_{B_{\delta}^{c}(y)\cap B_{\delta_{0}}(y)}\frac{e^{-ikv(y_{0})t}}{v^{\prime}(y_{0})}\left(\partial_{y_{0}}^{2}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{-}(y,y_{0})-\partial_{y_{0}}^{2}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{+}(y,y_{0})\right)\mathrm{d}y_{0}

\displaystyle\quad+\int_{B_{\delta_{0}}^{c}(y)}\frac{e^{-ikv(y_{0})t}}{v^{\prime}(y_{0})}\left(\partial_{y_{0}}^{2}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{-}(y,y_{0})-\partial_{y_{0}}^{2}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{+}(y,y_{0})\right)\mathrm{d}y_{0}

\displaystyle={\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,2,2,1}+{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,2,2,2}

where
B_{\delta_{0}}^{c}(y)=(\vartheta_{1},\vartheta_{2})\setminus B_{\delta_{0}}(y)
. Firstly, for
{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,2,2,1}
we see from the local decomposition and estimates of Proposition
12.14
that

\displaystyle|\lim_{\varepsilon\rightarrow 0}{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,2,2,1}|\lesssim k^{-\frac{3}{2}}\left(\log\left(\frac{\delta_{0}}{\delta}\right)+(k\delta)^{-\frac{1}{2}-\mu(y)}\right){\mathcal{S}}_{k,2}

because

\displaystyle\int_{B_{\delta}^{c}(y)\cap B_{\delta_{0}}(y)}(k|y-y_{0}|)^{-\frac{1}{2}-\mu_{0}}\mathrm{d}y_{0}\lesssim-k^{-1}\log\left(\frac{\delta_{0}}{\delta}\right)

while for
{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,2,2,2}
we use the global pointwise estimates of Proposition
12.14
and
12.17
to deduce that

\displaystyle|\lim_{\varepsilon\rightarrow 0}{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,2,2,2}|\lesssim k^{-\frac{1}{2}}{\mathcal{S}}_{k,2}.

Consequently,
|\lim_{\varepsilon\rightarrow 0}{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,2,2}|\lesssim k^{-\frac{1}{2}}\left(\log\left(\frac{\delta_{0}}{\delta}\right)+(k\delta)^{-\frac{1}{2}-\mu(y)}\right){\mathcal{S}}_{k,2}
.

\diamond

Estimates for
{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,2,3}
.
Arguing as for
{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,2,2}
, Propositions
12.7
and
12.10
give

\displaystyle|{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,2,3}|\lesssim k^{-\frac{1}{2}}(k\delta)^{\frac{1}{2}-\mu(y)}{\mathcal{S}}_{k,1}.

\bullet

End of proof.

Gathering the estimates we obtain

\displaystyle|\psi_{k}(t,y)|

\displaystyle\lesssim(kt)^{-1}k^{-\frac{3}{2}}(k\delta)^{\frac{1}{2}-\mu(y)}{\mathcal{S}}_{k,1}+k^{-\frac{1}{2}}(kt)^{-2}\left(-\log\left(\frac{\delta_{0}}{\delta}\right)+(k\delta)^{-\frac{1}{2}-\mu(y)}\right){\mathcal{S}}_{k,2}

Setting
\delta=\frac{\delta_{0}}{4t}
, we obtain the desired result.
∎

In the non-stratified regime we obtain optimal Euler-type decay rates.

Proposition 13.4
.

Let
k\geq 1
and
y\in[0,\vartheta_{1}]\cup[\vartheta_{2},2]
. Then,

\displaystyle|\psi_{k}(t,y)|\lesssim k^{-\frac{5}{2}}t^{-2}{\mathcal{S}}_{k,2}

for all
t\geq 1
.

Proof.

We assume
y\in[\vartheta_{2},1]
. Let
\delta_{0}=\frac{3\beta}{k}
. Integrating by parts once more,

\displaystyle\psi_{k}(t,y)

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{(ikt)^{2}}\frac{1}{2\pi i}\lim_{\varepsilon\rightarrow 0}\frac{e^{-ikv(y_{0})t}}{v^{\prime}(y_{0})}\left(\partial_{y_{0}}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{-}(y,y_{0})-\partial_{y_{0}}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{+}(y,y_{0})\right)\Big|_{y_{0}=\vartheta_{1}}^{y_{0}=\vartheta_{2}}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{(ikt)^{2}}\frac{1}{2\pi i}\lim_{\varepsilon\rightarrow 0}\int_{(\vartheta_{1},\vartheta_{2})\cap B_{\delta_{0}}(y)}{e^{-ikv(y_{0})t}}\partial_{y_{0}}\left(\frac{1}{v^{\prime}(y_{0})}\left(\partial_{y_{0}}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{-}(y,y_{0})-\partial_{y_{0}}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{+}(y,y_{0})\right)\right)\mathrm{d}y_{0}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{(ikt)^{2}}\frac{1}{2\pi i}\lim_{\varepsilon\rightarrow 0}\int_{(\vartheta_{1},\vartheta_{2})\cap B_{\delta_{0}}^{c}(y)}{e^{-ikv(y_{0})t}}\partial_{y_{0}}\left(\frac{1}{v^{\prime}(y_{0})}\left(\partial_{y_{0}}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{-}(y,y_{0})-\partial_{y_{0}}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{+}(y,y_{0})\right)\right)\mathrm{d}y_{0}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{(ikt)^{2}}\frac{1}{2\pi i}\lim_{\varepsilon\rightarrow 0}\left(-{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,1}+{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,2}+{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,3}\right).

We argue for each term. Using the global pointwise estimates of Proposition
12.7
we deduce that

\displaystyle\left|{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,1}\right|\lesssim k^{-\frac{1}{2}}{\mathcal{S}}_{k,1}.

With the local decomposition and estimates of Propositions
12.7
and
12.14
, together with the support assumptions on
{\mathcal{P}}(y)
and
v^{\prime\prime}(y)
and also on
\omega_{k}^{0}
and
\varrho_{k}^{0}
, we see that

\displaystyle\left|{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,2}\right|\lesssim k^{-\frac{3}{2}}{\mathcal{S}}_{k,2}.

Lastly, we use the global pointwise estimates of Propositions
12.7
and
12.14
to obtain

\displaystyle\left|{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,2}\right|\lesssim k^{-\frac{1}{2}}{\mathcal{S}}_{k,2}.

Gathering the estimates, the proof is concluded.

Combining it with the estimate for
{\mathcal{I}}_{1}
and choosing
\delta=\frac{\delta_{0}}{4t}
we obtain the desired result.
∎

The time decay rates of Proposition
13.3
degenerate as
y\rightarrow\vartheta_{1}
or
y\rightarrow\vartheta_{2}
, where the stratification of the background density vanishes and where
\psi_{k}(t,y)
decays faster according to Proposition
13.4
. We next provide a more precise description for
\psi_{k}(t,y)
for
y
near
\vartheta_{n}
, which allows us to locally improve the inviscid damping decay rates. To that purpose, we observe that

\displaystyle\psi_{k}(t,y)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2\pi i}\lim_{\varepsilon\rightarrow 0}\int_{\vartheta_{1}}^{\vartheta_{2}}e^{-ikv(y_{0})t}\left(\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{-}(y,y_{0})-\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{+}(y,y_{0})\right)v^{\prime}(y_{0})\mathrm{d}y_{0}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{ikt}\frac{1}{2\pi i}\lim_{\varepsilon\rightarrow 0}\int_{\vartheta_{1}}^{\vartheta_{2}}e^{-ikv(y_{0})t}\left(\partial_{y_{0}}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{-}(y,y_{0})-\partial_{y_{0}}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{+}(y,y_{0})\right)\mathrm{d}y_{0}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{ikt}\frac{1}{2\pi i}\lim_{\varepsilon\rightarrow 0}\int_{\vartheta_{1}}^{\vartheta_{2}}e^{-ikv(y_{0})t}\left(\varphi_{1,k,\varepsilon}^{-}(y,y_{0})-\varphi_{1,k,\varepsilon}^{+}(y,y_{0})\right)\mathrm{d}y_{0}

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{1}{ikv^{\prime}(y)t}\frac{1}{2\pi i}\lim_{\varepsilon\rightarrow 0}\int_{\vartheta_{1}}^{\vartheta_{2}}e^{-ikv(y_{0})t}\partial_{y}\left(\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{-}(y,y_{0})-\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{+}(y,y_{0})\right)v^{\prime}(y_{0})\mathrm{d}y_{0}

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{1}{ikv^{\prime}(y)t}\frac{1}{2\pi i}\lim_{\varepsilon\rightarrow 0}\int_{\vartheta_{1}}^{\vartheta_{2}}e^{-ikv(y_{0})t}\partial_{y}\left(\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{-}(y,y_{0})-\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{+}(y,y_{0})\right)(v^{\prime}(y)-v^{\prime}(y_{0}))\mathrm{d}y_{0}

Hence, for

\displaystyle\psi_{k,\varepsilon}(t,y):=\frac{1}{2\pi i}\int_{\vartheta_{1}}^{\vartheta_{2}}e^{-ikv(y_{0})t}\left(\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{-}(y,y_{0})-\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{+}(y,y_{0})\right)v^{\prime}(y_{0})\mathrm{d}y_{0}

we have

\displaystyle ikv^{\prime}(y)t\psi_{k,\varepsilon}(t,y)+\partial_{y}\psi_{k,\varepsilon}(t,y)

\displaystyle=v^{\prime}(y)\int_{\vartheta_{1}}^{\vartheta_{2}}e^{-ikv(y_{0})t}\left(\varphi_{1,k,\varepsilon}^{-}(y,y_{0})-\varphi_{1,k,\varepsilon}^{+}(y,y_{0})\right)\mathrm{d}y_{0}

\displaystyle\quad-\int_{\vartheta_{1}}^{\vartheta_{2}}e^{-ikv(y_{0})t}\partial_{y}\left(\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{-}(y,y_{0})-\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{+}(y,y_{0})\right)(v^{\prime}(y)-v^{\prime}(y_{0}))\mathrm{d}y_{0}.

Integrating from
y
to
\vartheta_{2}
, we reach

\displaystyle e^{ikv(y)t}\psi_{k,\varepsilon}(t,y)

\displaystyle=e^{ikv(\vartheta_{2})t}\psi_{k,\varepsilon}(t,\vartheta_{2})

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{2\pi i}\int_{y}^{\vartheta_{2}}\int_{\vartheta_{1}}^{\vartheta_{2}}e^{-ik(v(y_{0})-v(z))t}\partial_{z}\left(\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{-}(z,y_{0})-\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{+}(z,y_{0})\right)(v^{\prime}(z)-v^{\prime}(y_{0}))\mathrm{d}y_{0}\mathrm{d}z

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{1}{2\pi i}\int_{y}^{\vartheta_{2}}v^{\prime}(z)\int_{\vartheta_{1}}^{\vartheta_{2}}e^{-ik(v(y_{0})-v(z))t}\left(\varphi_{1,k,\varepsilon}^{-}(z,y_{0})-\varphi_{1,k,\varepsilon}^{+}(z,y_{0})\right)\mathrm{d}y_{0}\mathrm{d}z

\displaystyle:=e^{ikv(\vartheta_{2})t}\psi_{k,\varepsilon}(t,\vartheta_{2})+\psi_{1,k,\varepsilon}(t,y)+\psi_{2,k,\varepsilon}(t,y)

for all
y\in(\varpi_{2,2},\vartheta_{2})
. We likewise reach similar expressions for
y\in(\vartheta_{1},\varpi_{1,1})
, where now we integrate from
\vartheta_{1}
to
y
. As a result, we now have

Proposition 13.5
.

Let
k\geq 1
and
t\geq 1
. Then,

\displaystyle\left|\psi_{k}(t,y)\right|\lesssim k^{-\frac{3}{2}}\min\left(t^{-\frac{3}{2}+\mu(y)},t^{-2}+(\vartheta_{2}-y)t^{-1}\right){\mathcal{S}}_{k,2},

for all
y\in(\varpi_{2,2},\vartheta_{2})
.

The proposition is a consequence of Proposition
13.4
and Lemmas
13.6
and
13.7
below.

Lemma 13.6
.

Let
k\geq 1
and
y\in(\varpi_{2,2},\vartheta_{2})
. Then,

\displaystyle\left|\psi_{1,k,\varepsilon}(t,y)\right|\lesssim k^{-\frac{5}{2}}\left(t^{-2}+(\vartheta_{2}-y)t^{-1}\right){\mathcal{S}}_{k,2}

uniformly for all
0<\varepsilon<\varepsilon_{*}
.

Proof.

We integrate by parts once in
z\in(y,2)
to write

\displaystyle\psi_{1,k,\varepsilon}(t,y)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{ikt}\frac{1}{2\pi i}\int_{\vartheta_{1}}^{\vartheta_{2}}\frac{e^{-ik(v(y_{0})-v(z))t}}{v^{\prime}(z)}\partial_{y}\left(\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{-}(z,y_{0})-\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{+}(z,y_{0})\right)(v^{\prime}(z)-v^{\prime}(y_{0}))\mathrm{d}y_{0}\Big|_{z=y}^{z=\vartheta_{2}}

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{1}{ikt}\frac{1}{2\pi i}\int_{y}^{1}\int_{\vartheta_{1}}^{\vartheta_{2}}\frac{e^{-ik(v(y_{0})-v(z))t}}{v^{\prime}(z)}\left(\partial_{z}^{2}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{-}(z,y_{0})-\partial_{z}^{2}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{+}(z,y_{0})\right)(v^{\prime}(z)-v^{\prime}(y_{0}))\mathrm{d}y_{0}\mathrm{d}z

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{1}{ikt}\frac{1}{2\pi i}\int_{y}^{1}\int_{\vartheta_{1}}^{\vartheta_{2}}e^{-ik(v(y_{0})-v(z))t}\partial_{z}\left(\frac{v^{\prime}(z)-v^{\prime}(y_{0})}{v^{\prime}(z)}\right)\partial_{z}\left(\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{-}(z,y_{0})-\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{+}(z,y_{0})\right)\mathrm{d}y_{0}\mathrm{d}z

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{ikt}\frac{1}{2\pi i}\left({\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,1}-{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,2}-{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,3}+{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,4}\right).

\bullet

Estimates for
{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,1}
and
{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,2}
.
Let
z=\vartheta_{2}
for
j=1
and
z=y
for
j=2
, we have

\displaystyle{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,j}

\displaystyle=\int_{\vartheta_{1}}^{\vartheta_{2}}\frac{e^{-ik(v(y_{0})-v(z))t}}{v^{\prime}(z)}\partial_{y}\left(\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{-}(z,y_{0})-\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{+}(z,y_{0})\right)(v^{\prime}(z)-v^{\prime}(y_{0}))\mathrm{d}y_{0}

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{ikt}\frac{e^{-ik(v(y_{0})-v(z))t}}{v^{\prime}(z)v^{\prime}(y_{0})}\partial_{y}\left(\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{-}(z,y_{0})-\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{+}(z,y_{0})\right)(v^{\prime}(z)-v^{\prime}(y_{0}))\Big|_{y_{0}=\vartheta_{1}}^{y_{0}=\vartheta_{2}}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{ikt}\int_{\vartheta_{1}}^{\vartheta_{2}}\frac{e^{-ik(v(y_{0})-v(z))t}}{v^{\prime}(z)}\partial_{y_{0}}\left(\frac{v^{\prime}(z)-v^{\prime}(y_{0})}{v^{\prime}(y_{0})}\partial_{y}\left(\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{-}(z,y_{0})-\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{+}(z,y_{0})\right)\right)\mathrm{d}y_{0}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{ikt}\left(-{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,j,1}+{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,j,2}+{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,j,3}\right).

From Proposition
12.2
we see
\left|\partial_{y}\left(\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{-}(z,y_{0})-\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{+}(z,y_{0})\right)(v^{\prime}(z)-v^{\prime}(y_{0}))\right|\lesssim k^{-\frac{3}{2}}{\mathcal{S}}_{k,1}
, which yields

\displaystyle|{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,j,1}|+|{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,j,2}|\lesssim k^{-\frac{3}{2}}{\mathcal{S}}_{k,1}.

For
{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,j,3}
, we next split

\displaystyle{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,j,3}

\displaystyle=\int_{I_{3}(y)}\frac{e^{-ik(v(y_{0})-v(z))t}}{v^{\prime}(z)}\partial_{y_{0}}\left(\frac{v^{\prime}(z)-v^{\prime}(y_{0})}{v^{\prime}(y_{0})}\partial_{y}\left(\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{-}(z,y_{0})-\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{+}(z,y_{0})\right)\right)\mathrm{d}y_{0}

\displaystyle\quad+\int_{I_{3}^{c}(y)}\frac{e^{-ik(v(y_{0})-v(z))t}}{v^{\prime}(z)}\partial_{y_{0}}\left(\frac{v^{\prime}(z)-v^{\prime}(y_{0})}{v^{\prime}(y_{0})}\partial_{y}\left(\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{-}(z,y_{0})-\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{+}(z,y_{0})\right)\right)\mathrm{d}y_{0}

\displaystyle={\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,j,3,1}+{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,j,3,2}.

where now
I_{3}^{c}(y)=(\vartheta_{1},\vartheta_{2})\setminus I_{3}(y)
. Using the local decomposition and bounds of Propositions
12.2
and
12.18
, noting that
(k|z-y_{0}|)^{-\frac{1}{2}-\mu_{0})}\lesssim(k|z-y_{0}|)^{-\frac{1}{2}-\mu(z)}
because
(k|z-y_{0}|)\lesssim 1
for all
y_{0}\in I_{3}(z)
, we have

\displaystyle|{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,j,3,1}|

\displaystyle\lesssim k^{-\frac{1}{2}}{\mathcal{S}}_{k,2}\int_{I_{3}(z)}\left(1+\left({\mathcal{P}}(z)+(1-2\mu(z))\right)(k|z-y_{0}|)^{-\frac{1}{2}-\mu(z)}\right)\mathrm{d}y_{0}

\displaystyle\lesssim k^{-\frac{3}{2}}{\mathcal{S}}_{k,2}

since
\frac{{\mathcal{P}}(z)}{\frac{1}{2}-\mu(z)}=\frac{\frac{1}{2}+\mu(z)}{v^{\prime}(z)}
is uniformly bounded. Likewise the global pointwise bounds of Propositions
12.2
and
12.18
yield

\displaystyle|{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,j,3,2}|\lesssim k^{-\frac{1}{2}}{\mathcal{S}}_{k,2}

and we conclude that
|{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,j}|\lesssim k^{-\frac{1}{2}}(kt)^{-1}{\mathcal{S}}_{k,2}
, for
j=1,2
.

\bullet

Estimates for
{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,3}
.

We first write

\displaystyle{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,3}=\int_{y}^{\vartheta_{2}}\frac{e^{ikv(z)t}}{v^{\prime}(z)}\widetilde{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,3}(z)\mathrm{d}z,

where

\displaystyle\widetilde{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,3}(z)

\displaystyle:=\int_{\vartheta_{1}}^{\vartheta_{2}}e^{-ikv(y_{0})t}\left(\partial_{z}^{2}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{-}(z,y_{0})-\partial_{z}^{2}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{+}(z,y_{0})\right)(v^{\prime}(z)-v^{\prime}(y_{0}))\mathrm{d}y_{0}

\displaystyle=\int_{\vartheta_{1}}^{\vartheta_{2}}e^{-ikv(y_{0})t}V_{k,\varepsilon}^{-}(z,y_{0})\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{-}(z,y_{0})(v^{\prime}(z)-v^{\prime}(y_{0}))\mathrm{d}y_{0}

\displaystyle\quad-\int_{\vartheta_{1}}^{\vartheta_{2}}e^{-ikv(y_{0})t}V_{k,\varepsilon}^{+}(z,y_{0})\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{+}(z,y_{0})(v^{\prime}(z)-v^{\prime}(y_{0}))\mathrm{d}y_{0}

\displaystyle\quad+w_{k}^{0}(z)\int_{\vartheta_{1}}^{\vartheta_{2}}e^{-ikv(y_{0})t}\frac{2i\varepsilon(v^{\prime}(z)-v^{\prime}(y_{0}))}{(v(z)-v(y_{0}))^{2}+\varepsilon^{2}}\mathrm{d}y_{0}

\displaystyle=:\widetilde{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,3}^{-}-\widetilde{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,3}^{+}+\widehat{{\mathcal{I}}}_{\varepsilon,3},

where we defined

\displaystyle V_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(z,y_{0})=k^{2}+\frac{v^{\prime\prime}(z)}{v(z)-v(y_{0})\pm i\varepsilon}-\frac{{\mathcal{P}}(z)}{(v(z)-v(y_{0})\pm i\varepsilon)^{2}}.

The dominated convergence theorem directly yields
\lim_{\varepsilon\rightarrow 0}\widehat{{\mathcal{I}}}_{\varepsilon,3}=0
so we focus on
\widetilde{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,3}^{\pm}
. Set
\delta_{0}=\frac{3\beta}{k}
and
\delta\in(0,\frac{\delta_{0}}{2})
. Let
B_{r}:=B_{r}(z)
the ball of radius
r>0
centred at
z
. We further set

\displaystyle\widetilde{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,3,1}^{\pm}=\int_{B_{\delta}(z)\cap(\vartheta_{1},\vartheta_{2})}e^{-ikv(y_{0})t}V_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(z,y_{0})\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(z,y_{0})(v^{\prime}(z)-v^{\prime}(y_{0}))\mathrm{d}y_{0}

and

\displaystyle\widetilde{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,3,2}^{\pm}=\int_{B_{\delta}^{c}(z)\cap(\vartheta_{1},\vartheta_{2})}e^{-ikv(y_{0})t}V_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(z,y_{0})\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(z,y_{0})(v^{\prime}(z)-v^{\prime}(y_{0}))\mathrm{d}y_{0}

so that
\widetilde{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,3}^{\pm}=\widetilde{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,3,1}^{\pm}+\widetilde{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,3,1}^{\pm}
. From Proposition
12.2
we have

\displaystyle|\widetilde{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,3,1}^{\pm}|

\displaystyle=\left|\int_{z-\delta}^{z+\delta}e^{-ikv(y_{0})t}V_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(z,y_{0})\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(z,y_{0})(v^{\prime}(z)-v^{\prime}(y_{0}))\mathrm{d}y_{0}\right|

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim k^{-\frac{1}{2}}{\mathcal{S}}_{k,2}\int_{z-\delta}^{z+\delta}\left(1+{\mathcal{P}}(z)\left((k|z-y_{0}|)^{-\frac{1}{2}+\mu_{0}}+(k|z-y_{0}|)^{-\frac{1}{2}-\mu_{0}}\right)\right)\mathrm{d}y_{0}

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim k^{-\frac{3}{2}}\left((k\delta)+(k\delta)^{\frac{1}{2}-\mu(z)}+(k\delta)^{\frac{1}{2}+\mu(z)}\right){\mathcal{S}}_{k,2}

Here we have used that
|\mu(z)-\mu_{0}||\log(k|z-y_{0}|)\lesssim k^{-1}
for
|z-y_{0}|\leq\delta\leq\frac{1}{2k}
so that

\displaystyle\eta^{-\frac{1}{2}+\mu_{0}}\lesssim\eta^{-\frac{1}{2}+\mu(z)},\quad\eta^{-\frac{1}{2}-\mu_{0}}\lesssim\eta^{-\frac{1}{2}-\mu(z)},

for
\eta=k|z-y_{0}|
and also that
\frac{{\mathcal{P}}(z)}{\frac{1}{2}-\mu(z)}=\frac{\frac{1}{2}+\mu(z)}{v^{\prime}(z)}\lesssim 1
. On the other hand, for
\widetilde{\mathcal{I}}_{3,2}
, we integrate by parts in
y_{0}\in(\vartheta_{1},\vartheta_{2})\cap B_{\delta}^{c}(z)
thus obtaining

\displaystyle\widetilde{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,3,2}^{\pm}

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{ikt}\frac{e^{-ikv(y_{0})t}}{v^{\prime}(y_{0})}V_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(z,y_{0})\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(z,y_{0})(v^{\prime}(z)-v^{\prime}(y_{0}))\Big|_{y_{0}\in\partial B_{\delta}^{c}\cap(\vartheta_{1},\vartheta_{2})}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{ikt}\int_{B_{\delta}^{c}(z)\cap B_{\delta_{0}}(z)\cap(\vartheta_{1},\vartheta_{2})}e^{-ikv(y_{0})t}\partial_{y_{0}}\left(\frac{v^{\prime}(z)-v^{\prime}(y_{0})}{v^{\prime}(y_{0})}V_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(z,y_{0})\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(z,y_{0})\right)\mathrm{d}y_{0}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{ikt}\int_{B_{\delta_{0}}^{c}(z)\cap(\vartheta_{1},\vartheta_{2})}e^{-ikv(y_{0})t}\partial_{y_{0}}\left(\frac{v^{\prime}(z)-v^{\prime}(y_{0})}{v^{\prime}(y_{0})}V_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(z,y_{0})\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(z,y_{0})\right)\mathrm{d}y_{0}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{ikt}\left(-\widetilde{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,3,2,1}^{\pm}+\widetilde{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,3,2,2}^{\pm}+\widetilde{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,3,2,3}^{\pm}\right)

We argue for each term.

\diamond

Estimates for
\widetilde{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,3,2,1}^{\pm}
.
If
y_{0}=\vartheta_{1}
or
y_{0}=\vartheta_{2}
and
y_{0}\in B_{\delta}^{c}(z)
we have
|v(z)-v(y_{0})\pm i\varepsilon|^{-1}\lesssim(k\delta)^{-1}
and thus from Lemma
11.6
and the dominated convergence theorem we have

\displaystyle\lim_{\varepsilon\rightarrow 0}\int_{y}^{\vartheta_{2}}e^{ikv(z)t}\frac{\widetilde{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,3,2,1}^{-}(z)-\widetilde{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,3,2,1}^{+}(z)}{v^{\prime}(z)}\mathrm{d}z=0.

On the other hand, for
y_{0}\in\partial B_{\delta}(y)\subset(\vartheta_{1},\vartheta_{2})
, we have
k|z-y_{0}|=\delta
and we appeal to the local bounds of Proposition
12.2
to have

\displaystyle\left|V_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(z,y_{0})\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(z,y_{0})(v^{\prime}(z)-v^{\prime}(y_{0}))\right|\lesssim k^{-\frac{3}{2}}\left(1+{\mathcal{P}}(z)(k\delta)^{-\frac{1}{2}-\mu(z)}\right){\mathcal{S}}_{k,1}

which then yields
|\widetilde{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,3,2,1}^{\pm}|\lesssim k^{-\frac{5}{2}}\left(1+{\mathcal{P}}(z)(k\delta)^{-\frac{1}{2}-\mu(z)}\right){\mathcal{S}}_{k,2}
, uniformly for all
0<\varepsilon<\varepsilon_{*}
.

\diamond

Estimates for
\widetilde{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,3,2,2}
.
For
y_{0}\in B_{\delta}^{c}(z)\cap B_{\delta_{0}}(z)\cap(\vartheta_{1},\vartheta_{2})
we use Propositions
12.2
and
12.7
to obtain

\displaystyle\left|\partial_{y_{0}}\left(\frac{v^{\prime}(z)-v^{\prime}(y_{0})}{v^{\prime}(y_{0})}V_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(z,y_{0})\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(z,y_{0})\right)\right|

\displaystyle\qquad\lesssim k^{-\frac{1}{2}}\left(1+|v^{\prime\prime}(z)|(k|z-y_{0}|)^{-\frac{1}{2}-\mu(z)}+{\mathcal{P}}(z)(k|z-y_{0}|)^{-\frac{3}{2}-\mu(z)}\right){\mathcal{S}}_{k,2}

and thus

\displaystyle|\widetilde{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,3,2,2}^{\pm}|

\displaystyle\lesssim k^{-\frac{1}{2}}{\mathcal{S}}_{k,2}\int_{B_{\delta}^{c}(z)\cap B_{\delta_{0}}(z)}\left(1+|v^{\prime\prime}(z)|(k|z-y_{0}|)^{-\frac{1}{2}-\mu(z)}+{\mathcal{P}}(z)(k|z-y_{0}|)^{-\frac{3}{2}-\mu(z)}\right)\mathrm{d}y_{0}

\displaystyle\lesssim k^{-\frac{3}{2}}\left(1+(k\delta)^{\frac{1}{2}-\mu(z)}+{\mathcal{P}}(z)(k\delta)^{-\frac{1}{2}-\mu(z)}\right){\mathcal{S}}_{k,2}

since
\frac{|v^{\prime\prime}(z)|}{\frac{1}{2}-\mu(z)}\lesssim 1
due to the compact support of
v^{\prime\prime}
.

\diamond

Estimates for
\widetilde{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,3,2,3}^{\pm}
.
Now
y_{0}\in B_{\delta_{0}}^{c}(z)\cap(\vartheta_{1},\vartheta_{2})
and we can use the global pointwise estimates of Propositions
12.2
and
G.2
to obtain

\displaystyle\left|\partial_{y_{0}}\left(\frac{v^{\prime}(z)-v^{\prime}(y_{0})}{v^{\prime}(y_{0})}V_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(z,y_{0})\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(z,y_{0})\right)\right|\lesssim k^{-\frac{1}{2}}{\mathcal{S}}_{k,2}.

\diamond

Estimates for
{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,3}
.
Hence, we conclude that
|\widetilde{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,3,2}|\lesssim k^{-\frac{1}{2}}\left(1+{\mathcal{P}}(z)(k\delta)^{-\frac{1}{2}-\mu(z)}\right){\mathcal{S}}_{k,2}
. Together with the bound for
\widetilde{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,3,1}
, we obtain

\displaystyle|\widetilde{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,3}|

\displaystyle\lesssim k^{-\frac{3}{2}}\left((k\delta)+(k\delta)^{\frac{1}{2}-\mu(z)}\right){\mathcal{S}}_{k,2}+k^{-\frac{1}{2}}(kt)^{-1}\left(1+{\mathcal{P}}(z)(k\delta)^{-\frac{1}{2}-\mu(z)}\right){\mathcal{S}}_{k,2}.

Next, to obtain bounds on
{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,3}
. Optimizing in
k\delta
, we find that
k\delta=\frac{\beta}{t}
we have
\delta\leq\frac{\delta_{0}}{2}
for
t\geq 1
and

\displaystyle|\widetilde{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,3}|

\displaystyle\lesssim k^{-\frac{3}{2}}\left(t^{-1}+t^{-\frac{1}{2}+\mu(z)}\right){\mathcal{S}}_{k,2}

so that

\displaystyle\left|{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,3}\right|\lesssim k^{-\frac{3}{2}}(\vartheta_{2}-y)(1+t^{-1}){\mathcal{S}}_{k,2}

In fact, we can recover
t^{-\frac{1}{2}+\mu(y)}
at the expenses of a logarithmic loss. Indeed,

\displaystyle t^{-\frac{1}{2}+\mu(z)}\lesssim t^{-\frac{1}{2}+\mu(y)}+|z-y|\log(t)

for all
t\geq 1
, thus concluding that

\displaystyle\left|{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,3}\right|\lesssim k^{-\frac{3}{2}}(\vartheta_{2}-y)\left(t^{-1}+t^{-\frac{1}{2}+\mu(y)}+(\vartheta_{2}-y)\log(t)\right){\mathcal{S}}_{k,2}.

\bullet

Estimates for
{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,4}
.

As before we write

\displaystyle{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,4}=\int_{y}^{\vartheta_{2}}e^{ikv(z)t}\widetilde{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,4}(z)\mathrm{d}z

where

\displaystyle\widetilde{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,4}

\displaystyle=\int_{\vartheta_{1}}^{\vartheta_{2}}e^{-ikv(y_{0})t}\partial_{z}\left(\frac{v^{\prime}(z)-v^{\prime}(y_{0})}{v^{\prime}(z)}\right)\partial_{z}\left(\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{-}(z,y_{0})-\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{+}(z,y_{0})\right)\mathrm{d}y_{0}

\displaystyle=\int_{B_{\delta}(z)\cap(\vartheta_{1},\vartheta_{2})}e^{-ikv(y_{0})t}\partial_{z}\left(\frac{v^{\prime}(z)-v^{\prime}(y_{0})}{v^{\prime}(z)}\right)\partial_{z}\left(\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{-}(z,y_{0})-\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{+}(z,y_{0})\right)\mathrm{d}y_{0}

\displaystyle\quad+\int_{B_{\delta}^{c}(z)\cap(\vartheta_{1},\vartheta_{2})}e^{-ikv(y_{0})t}\partial_{z}\left(\frac{v^{\prime}(z)-v^{\prime}(y_{0})}{v^{\prime}(z)}\right)\partial_{z}\left(\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{-}(z,y_{0})-\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{+}(z,y_{0})\right)\mathrm{d}y_{0}

\displaystyle=\widetilde{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,4,1}+\widetilde{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,4,2}.

Next, Proposition
12.2
yields

\displaystyle|\widetilde{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,4,1}|

\displaystyle\lesssim k^{-\frac{1}{2}}{\mathcal{S}}_{k,1}\int_{z-\delta}^{z+\delta}\left(1+(k|z-y_{0}|)^{-\frac{1}{2}+\mu(z)}+(1-2\mu(z))(k|z-y_{0}|)^{-\frac{1}{2}-\mu(z)}\right)\mathrm{d}z

\displaystyle\lesssim k^{-\frac{3}{2}}\left((k\delta)+(k\delta)^{\frac{1}{2}-\mu(z)}\right){\mathcal{S}}_{k,1}.

For
\widetilde{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,4,2}
we integrate by parts again, obtaining

\displaystyle\widetilde{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,4,2}
