once we realise

\displaystyle 2k(1-2\gamma_{0})\xi^{-2\gamma_{0}}=\partial_{z}\left(\xi^{1-2\gamma_{0}}\right)

and we integrate by parts, we omit the details. Hence,

\displaystyle\|g_{7,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\|_{X_{K}^{1}}\lesssim\|f\|_{X_{k}}.

Concerning the
H_{k}^{1}(I_{3}(y_{0}))
estimate, we have that
g_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})
satisfies

\displaystyle\left({\Delta}_{k}-k^{2}+\frac{{\mathcal{J}}(y_{0})}{(y-y_{0}\pm i\varepsilon)^{2}}\right)g_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})=\frac{f(y)}{(v(y)-v(y_{0})\pm i\varepsilon)^{2}}

and thus the entanglement inequality provides

\displaystyle\|g_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\|_{H_{k}^{1}(I_{3}^{c}(y_{0}))}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{k^{2}}\left\|\frac{f(z,y_{0})}{(v(z)-v(y_{0})\pm i\varepsilon)^{2}}\right\|_{L^{2}(I_{2}^{c}(y_{0})\cap I_{3}(y_{0}))}+\frac{1}{k^{2}}\left\|\frac{f(z,y_{0})}{(v(z)-v(y_{0})\pm i\varepsilon)^{2}}\right\|_{L^{2}(I_{3}^{c}(y_{0}))}

\displaystyle\quad+\|g_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\|_{L^{2}(I_{2}^{c}(y_{0})\cap I_{3}(y_{0}))}

\displaystyle\lesssim k^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|f\|_{X_{k}^{1}}+\|f\|_{L^{2}(I_{3}^{c}(y_{0}))}

Here we have used that
\int_{a}^{b}|x^{-\frac{3}{2}\pm\gamma_{0}}|^{2}\mathrm{d}x
is uniformly bounded in
\gamma_{0}
for
0<\mu_{0}\leq\frac{1}{2}
for all
0<a<b<+\infty
. With this we finish the proof.
∎

We can combine Lemma
6.6
and Lemma
E.1
to obtain the following

Corollary E.2
.

Let
k\geq 1
and
y_{0}\in I_{S}\cup I_{W}
. Let
f\in X_{k}
and
h(z,y_{0})\in C^{2}
uniformly in
y_{0}
. Then,

\displaystyle\|R_{2,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}hf\|_{X_{k}}\lesssim\|f\|_{X_{k}},

uniformly for all
0<\varepsilon<\varepsilon_{*}
and all
y_{0}\in I_{S}\cup I_{W}
.

Proof.

It follows from Lemma
6.6
and Lemma
E.1
once we write

\displaystyle g_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}=R_{2,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}hf(y,y_{0})=h(y_{0})R_{2,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}f(y,y_{0})+R_{2,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}h_{1}f(y,y_{0}),

where we have defined
h_{1}(y,y_{0})=h(y,y_{0})-h(y_{0},y_{0})
, with
h_{1}(y_{0},y_{0})=0
and
h_{1}(\cdot,y_{0})\in C^{2}
uniformly in
y_{0}\in[0,2]
.
∎

We can dispense with the assumption that
f\in X_{k}
by instead assuming further regularity on
f
.

Lemma E.3
.

Let
k\geq 1
and
y_{0}\in I_{S}\cup I_{W}
. Let
f(y)\in H^{1}_{k}
and
h(y,y_{0})\in C^{2}_{y}
uniformly in
y_{0}
with
h(y_{0},y_{0})=0
. Then,

\displaystyle\|R_{2,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}hf\|_{X_{k}}\lesssim k^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|f\|_{H^{1}_{k}},

uniformly for all
0<\varepsilon<\varepsilon_{*}
and all
y_{0}\in I_{S}\cup I_{W}
.

Proof.

As usual, let
g_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}=R_{2,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}f(y,y_{0})
, let

\displaystyle g_{1,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0}):=\int_{I_{3}(y_{0})}{\mathcal{G}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0},z)\frac{h(z,y_{0})}{(v(z)-v(y_{0})\pm i\varepsilon)^{2}}f(z)\mathrm{d}z

and
g_{2,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}:=g_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}-g_{1,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}
. Appealing to Proposition
4.1
and Corollary
4.3
, we have
\|g_{2,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\|\lesssim k^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|f\|_{L^{2}}
. Integrating by parts,

\displaystyle g_{1,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})

\displaystyle=-\frac{{\mathcal{G}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0},z)}{v^{\prime}(z)}\frac{h(z,y_{0})}{v(z)-v(y_{0})\pm i\varepsilon}f(z)\Big|_{z\in\partial I_{3}(y_{0})}

\displaystyle\quad+\int_{I_{3}(y_{0})}\frac{{\mathcal{G}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0},z)}{v(z)-v(y_{0})\pm i\varepsilon}\left(\partial_{z}f(z)\frac{h(z,y_{0})}{v^{\prime}(z)}+f(z)\partial_{z}\left(\frac{h(z,y_{0})}{v^{\prime}(z)}\right)\right)\mathrm{d}z

\displaystyle\quad+\int_{I_{3}(y_{0})}\frac{h(z,y_{0})}{v^{\prime}(z)}\partial_{z}{\mathcal{G}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0},z)\frac{f(z)}{v(z)-v(y_{0})\pm i\varepsilon}\mathrm{d}z

\displaystyle=g_{3,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})+g_{4,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})+g_{5,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0}).

As before, Lemma
2.3
, Proposition
4.1
, Corollary
4.3
and the vanishing of
h(y_{0},y_{0})
provide

\displaystyle\|g_{3,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\|_{X_{k}^{1}}+\|g_{4,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\|_{X_{k}^{1}}\lesssim k^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|f\|_{H_{k}^{1}}.

We note here that for
g_{4,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}
we can argue as in Lemma
6.1
and Lemma
6.5
. Regarding
g_{5,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}
, we shall only study

\displaystyle g_{6,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})

\displaystyle:=2k\int_{I_{3}(y_{0})}\frac{h(z,y_{0})}{v^{\prime}(z)}\left({\mathcal{G}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)_{\mathsf{s}}(y,y_{0},z)\frac{\xi^{-\frac{1}{2}-\gamma_{0}}}{v(z)-v(y_{0})\pm i\varepsilon}f(z)\mathrm{d}z

\displaystyle=(2k)^{2}\int_{I_{3}(y_{0})}\frac{h(z,y_{0})}{v^{\prime}(y_{0})v^{\prime}(z)}\left({\mathcal{G}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)_{\mathsf{s}}(y,y_{0},z)f(z)\xi^{-\frac{3}{2}-\gamma_{0}}\mathrm{d}z

\displaystyle\quad-2k\int_{I_{3}(y_{0})}\frac{h(z,y_{0})}{v^{\prime}(z)}\left({\mathcal{G}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)_{\mathsf{s}}(y,y_{0},z)f(z)\xi^{-\frac{1}{2}-\gamma_{0}}\mathrm{V}_{1,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(z,y_{0})\mathrm{d}z

\displaystyle=g_{7,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})-g_{8,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}.

Since
V_{1,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\in L^{\infty}_{z}
uniformly in
y_{0}
and
\varepsilon>0
, we readily have
\|g_{8,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\|_{X_{k}^{1}}\lesssim k^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|f\|_{L^{2}}
thanks to the vanishing properties of
h(\cdot,y_{0})
. On the other hand, integrating by parts in
g_{7,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}
we reach

\displaystyle g_{7,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})

\displaystyle=-\frac{4k}{1+2\gamma_{0}}\frac{h(z,y_{0})}{v^{\prime}(y_{0})v^{\prime}(z)}\left({\mathcal{G}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)_{\mathsf{s}}(y,y_{0},z)f(z)\xi^{-\frac{1}{2}-\gamma_{0}}\Big|_{z\in\partial I_{3}(y_{0})}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{4k}{1+2\gamma_{0}}\int_{I_{3}(y_{0})}\frac{h(z,y_{0})}{v^{\prime}(y_{0})v^{\prime}(z)}f(z)\partial_{z}\left({\mathcal{G}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)_{\mathsf{s}}(y,y_{0},z)\xi^{-\frac{1}{2}-\gamma_{0}}\mathrm{d}z

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{4k}{1+2\gamma_{0}}\int_{I_{3}(y_{0})}\left({\mathcal{G}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)_{\mathsf{s}}(y,y_{0},z)\partial_{z}\left(f(z)\frac{h(z,y_{0})}{v^{\prime}(y_{0})v^{\prime}(z)}\right)\xi^{-\frac{1}{2}-\gamma_{0}}\mathrm{d}z.

The solid term is bounded as usual. For the first integral, from Corollary
4.3
we note that

\displaystyle\left|4k\frac{h(z,y_{0})}{v^{\prime}(y_{0})v^{\prime}(z)}f(z)\partial_{z}\left({\mathcal{G}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)_{\mathsf{s}}(y,y_{0},z)\xi^{-\frac{1}{2}-\gamma_{0}}\right|\lesssim|f(z)||\xi|^{\frac{3}{2}-\gamma_{0}}

which is clearly integrable. For the second integral,

\displaystyle\frac{4k}{1+2\gamma_{0}}

\displaystyle\int_{I_{3}(y_{0})}\left({\mathcal{G}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)_{\mathsf{s}}(y,y_{0},z)\partial_{z}\left(f(z)\frac{h(z,y_{0})}{v^{\prime}(y_{0})v^{\prime}(z)}\right)\xi^{-\frac{1}{2}-\gamma_{0}}\mathrm{d}z

\displaystyle=\frac{4k}{1+2\gamma_{0}}\int_{I_{3}(y_{0})}\left({\mathcal{G}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)_{\mathsf{s}}(y,y_{0},z)\frac{h(z,y_{0})}{v^{\prime}(y_{0})v^{\prime}(z)}\partial_{z}f(z)\xi^{-\frac{1}{2}-\gamma_{0}}\mathrm{d}z

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{4k}{1+2\gamma_{0}}\int_{I_{3}(y_{0})}\left({\mathcal{G}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)_{\mathsf{s}}(y,y_{0},z)f(z)\partial_{z}\left(\frac{h(z,y_{0})}{v^{\prime}(y_{0})v^{\prime}(z)}\right)\xi^{-\frac{1}{2}-\gamma_{0}}\mathrm{d}z

with

\displaystyle\left|4kh(z,y_{0})\xi^{-\frac{1}{2}-\gamma_{0}}\right|\lesssim|\xi|^{\frac{1}{2}-\mu_{0}}\lesssim 1+|\log|\xi||\in L^{2}(I_{3}(y_{0}))

while we further integrate by parts to get

\displaystyle\frac{4k}{1+2\gamma_{0}}

\displaystyle\int_{I_{3}(y_{0})}\left({\mathcal{G}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)_{\mathsf{s}}(y,y_{0},z)f(z)\partial_{z}\left(\frac{h(z,y_{0})}{v^{\prime}(y_{0})v^{\prime}(z)}\right)\xi^{-\frac{1}{2}-\gamma_{0}}\mathrm{d}z

\displaystyle=\frac{2}{1+2\gamma_{0}}\left({\mathcal{G}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)_{\mathsf{s}}(y,y_{0},z)f(z)\partial_{z}\left(\frac{h(z,y_{0})}{v^{\prime}(y_{0})v^{\prime}(z)}\right)\frac{\xi^{\frac{1}{2}-\gamma_{0}}-1}{1-2\gamma_{0}}\Big|_{z\in\partial I_{3}(y_{0})}

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{2}{1+2\gamma_{0}}\int_{I_{3}(y_{0})}\partial_{z}\left(\left({\mathcal{G}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)_{\mathsf{s}}(y,y_{0},z)f(z)\partial_{z}\left(\frac{h(z,y_{0})}{v^{\prime}(y_{0})v^{\prime}(z)}\right)\right)\frac{\xi^{\frac{1}{2}-\gamma_{0}}-1}{1-2\gamma_{0}}\mathrm{d}z

Since

\displaystyle\left|\frac{\xi^{1-2\gamma_{0}}-1}{(1-2\gamma_{0})}\right|\lesssim|\log|\xi||\in L^{2}(I_{3}(y_{0})),

confer Lemma
A.1
, the solid term and the integral can then be bounded in
X_{k}^{1}
using Proposition
4.1
, Corollary
4.3
and
f\in H^{1}_{k}
, we omit the details.
∎

E.2.
Third order operator estimates

We define

\displaystyle R_{3,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}f(y,y_{0})=\int_{0}^{2}{\mathcal{G}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0},z)\frac{f(z)}{(v(z)-v(y_{0})\pm i\varepsilon)^{3}}\mathrm{d}z.

The first result is the following.

Lemma E.4
.

Let
k\geq 1
and
y_{0}\in I_{S}\cup I_{W}
. Let
f(y,y_{0})\in X_{k}
and
h(y,y_{0})\in C^{2}_{y}
uniformly in
y_{0}
with
h(y_{0},y_{0})=0
. Then,

\displaystyle\|R_{3,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}hf\|_{X_{k}}\lesssim\|f\|_{X_{k}},

uniformly for all
0<\varepsilon<\varepsilon_{*}
and all
y_{0}\in I_{S}\cup I_{W}
.

Proof.

We shall only focus on the most singular contribution of
g_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})=R_{3,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})hf(y,y_{0})
, namely

\displaystyle g_{1,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}

\displaystyle:=\int_{I_{3}(y_{0})}{\mathcal{G}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0},z)\frac{h(z,y_{0})f(z,y_{0})}{(v(z)-v(y_{0})\pm i\varepsilon)^{3}}\mathrm{d}z

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{2}\frac{{\mathcal{G}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0},z)}{v^{\prime}(z)}\frac{h(z,y_{0})f(z,y_{0})}{(v(z)-v(y_{0})\pm i\varepsilon)^{2}}\Big|_{z\in\partial I_{3}(y_{0})}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{2}\int_{I_{3}(y_{0})}{\mathcal{G}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0},z)\frac{f(z,y_{0})}{(v(z)-v(y_{0})\pm i\varepsilon)^{2}}\partial_{z}\left(\frac{h(z,y_{0})}{v^{\prime}(z)}\right)\mathrm{d}z

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{2}\int_{I_{3}(y_{0})}\frac{h(z,y_{0})}{v^{\prime}(z)}\frac{\partial_{z}\left({\mathcal{G}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0},z)f(z,y_{0})\right)}{(v(z)-v(y_{0})\pm i\varepsilon)^{2}}\mathrm{d}z

\displaystyle=g_{2,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})+g_{3,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})+g_{4,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0}).

As usual, Proposition
4.1
and Corollary
4.3
show
\|g_{2,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\|_{X_{k}^{1}}\lesssim k^{-1}\|f\|_{X_{k}^{1}}
. Regarding
g_{3,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})
, we appeal to Corollary
E.2
to obtain
\|g_{3,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\|_{X_{k}^{1}}\lesssim\|f\|_{X_{k}}
. For
g_{4,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}
both
{\mathcal{G}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0},\cdot)\in X_{k}^{1}
and
f(\cdot,y_{0})\in X_{k}^{1}
, we use (
6.4
) and we argue for the most singular contributions

\displaystyle g_{5,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\int_{I_{3}(y_{0})}\frac{h(z,y_{0})}{v^{\prime}(z)}\frac{\partial_{z}\left(\left({\mathcal{G}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)_{\mathsf{s}}(y,y_{0},z)f_{\mathsf{s}}(z,y_{0})\right)}{(v(z)-v(y_{0})\pm i\varepsilon)^{2}}\xi^{1-2\gamma_{0}}\mathrm{d}z

\displaystyle\quad+k(1-2\gamma_{0})\int_{I_{3}(y_{0})}\frac{h(z,y_{0})}{v^{\prime}(z)}\frac{\left({\mathcal{G}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)_{\mathsf{s}}(y,y_{0},z)f_{\mathsf{s}}(z,y_{0})}{(v(z)-v(y_{0})\pm i\varepsilon)^{2}}\xi^{-2\gamma_{0}}\mathrm{d}z

\displaystyle=g_{6,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})+g_{7,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0}).

Given the vanishing of
h(y_{0},y_{0})
, Proposition
4.1
, the bounds
\left\|\partial_{z}\left({\mathcal{G}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)_{\mathsf{s}}(\cdot,y_{0},z)\right\|_{X_{k}^{1}}\lesssim|\xi|^{\frac{1}{2}}
from Corollary
4.3
and
\left|\partial_{z}f_{\mathsf{s}}(z,y_{0})\right|\lesssim|\xi|^{\frac{1}{2}}k\|f\|_{X_{k}^{1}}
, we have

\displaystyle\|g_{6,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\|_{X_{k}^{1}}\lesssim k\|f\|_{X_{k}^{1}}\left(\int_{I_{3}(y_{0})}|\xi|^{\frac{1}{2}-2\mu_{0}}\mathrm{d}z\right)\lesssim\|f\|_{X_{k}^{1}}

since
x^{\frac{1}{2}-2\mu_{0}}=x^{-\frac{1}{2}}x^{1-2\mu_{0}}=x^{-\frac{1}{2}}\left(1+(1-2\mu_{0})\log(x)\mathcal{Q}_{\mu_{0}}(x)\right)
is locally integrable. We now address
g_{7,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}
. Integrating by parts once more,

\displaystyle g_{7,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})

\displaystyle=-k(1-2\gamma_{0})\frac{h(z,y_{0})}{(v^{\prime}(z))^{2}}\frac{\left({\mathcal{G}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)_{\mathsf{s}}(y,y_{0},z)f_{\mathsf{s}}(z,y_{0})}{v(z)-v(y_{0})\pm i\varepsilon}\xi^{-2\gamma_{0}}\Big|_{z\in\partial I_{3}(y_{0})}

\displaystyle\quad+k(1-2\gamma_{0})\int_{I_{3}(y_{0})}\frac{h(z,y_{0})}{(v^{\prime}(z))^{2}}\frac{\partial_{z}\left(\left({\mathcal{G}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)_{\mathsf{s}}(y,y_{0},z)f_{\mathsf{s}}(z,y_{0})\right)}{v(z)-v(y_{0})\pm i\varepsilon}\xi^{-2\gamma_{0}}\mathrm{d}z

\displaystyle\quad+k(1-2\gamma_{0})\int_{I_{3}(y_{0})}\partial_{z}\left(\frac{h(z,y_{0})}{(v^{\prime}(z))^{2}}\right)\frac{\left({\mathcal{G}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)_{\mathsf{s}}(y,y_{0},z)f_{\mathsf{s}}(z,y_{0})}{v(z)-v(y_{0})\pm i\varepsilon}\xi^{-2\gamma_{0}}\mathrm{d}z

\displaystyle\quad-4k^{2}\gamma_{0}(1-2\gamma_{0})\int_{I_{3}(y_{0})}\frac{h(z,y_{0})}{(v^{\prime}(z))^{2}}\frac{\left({\mathcal{G}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)_{\mathsf{s}}(y,y_{0},z)f_{\mathsf{s}}(z,y_{0})}{v(z)-v(y_{0})\pm i\varepsilon}\xi^{-1-2\gamma_{0}}\mathrm{d}z

\displaystyle=\sum_{j=8}^{11}g_{j,k\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})

We can estimate the boundary term
g_{8,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}
in
X_{k}^{1}
as usual. The term
g_{9,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}
is bounded following the same strategy as for
g_{6,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}
, taking advantage of the vanishing of
h(z,y_{0})
, of
\left\|\partial_{z}\left({\mathcal{G}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)_{\mathsf{s}}(\cdot,y_{0},z)\right\|_{X_{k}^{1}}\lesssim|\xi|^{\frac{1}{2}}
from Corollary
4.3
and
\left|\partial_{z}f_{\mathsf{s}}(z,y_{0})\right|\lesssim|\xi|^{\frac{1}{2}}k\|f\|_{X_{k}^{1}}
. For
g_{10,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}
, we write

\displaystyle g_{10,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})

\displaystyle=\frac{2k^{2}(1-2\gamma_{0})}{v^{\prime}(y_{0})}\int_{I_{3}(y_{0})}\partial_{z}\left(\frac{h(z,y_{0})}{(v^{\prime}(z))^{2}}\right)\left({\mathcal{G}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)_{\mathsf{s}}(y,y_{0},z)f_{\mathsf{s}}(z,y_{0})\xi^{-1-2\gamma_{0}}\mathrm{d}z

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{k(1-2\gamma_{0})}{v^{\prime}(y_{0})}\int_{I_{3}(y_{0})}\partial_{z}\left(\frac{h(z,y_{0})}{(v^{\prime}(z))^{2}}\right)\left({\mathcal{G}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)_{\mathsf{s}}(y,y_{0},z)f_{\mathsf{s}}(z,y_{0})\mathrm{V}_{1,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(z,y_{0})\xi^{-2\gamma_{0}}\mathrm{d}z

\displaystyle=g_{12,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})+g_{13,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0}).

Since
(1-2\mu_{0})\int_{0}^{3\beta^{2}}x^{-2\mu_{0}}\mathrm{d}x\lesssim 1
, we readily have
\|g_{13,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\|_{X_{k}^{1}}\lesssim\|f\|_{X_{k}^{1}}
. For
g_{12,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}
, we integrate once more to get

\displaystyle g_{12,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}

\displaystyle=-\frac{k(1-2\gamma_{0})}{v^{\prime}(y_{0})2\gamma_{0}}\partial_{z}\left(\frac{h(z,y_{0})}{(v^{\prime}(z))^{2}}\right)\left({\mathcal{G}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)_{\mathsf{s}}(y,y_{0},z)f_{\mathsf{s}}(z,y_{0})\xi^{-2\gamma_{0}}\Big|_{z\in\partial I_{3}(y_{0})}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{k(1-2\gamma_{0})}{2\gamma_{0}v^{\prime}(y_{0})}\int_{I_{3}(y_{0})}\partial_{z}\left(\frac{h(z,y_{0})}{(v^{\prime}(z))^{2}}\right)\partial_{z}\left(\left({\mathcal{G}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)_{\mathsf{s}}(y,y_{0},z)f_{\mathsf{s}}(z,y_{0})\right)\xi^{-2\gamma_{0}}\mathrm{d}z

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{k(1-2\gamma_{0})}{2\gamma_{0}v^{\prime}(y_{0})}\int_{I_{3}(y_{0})}\partial_{z}^{2}\left(\frac{h(z,y_{0})}{(v^{\prime}(z))^{2}}\right)\left({\mathcal{G}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)_{\mathsf{s}}(y,y_{0},z)f_{\mathsf{s}}(z,y_{0})\xi^{-2\gamma_{0}}\mathrm{d}z

where now each of them is bounded in
X_{k}^{1}
; we estimate the first integral as in
g_{6,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}
and the second integral as in
g_{13,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}
. The solid term is estimated as usual.

Finally, for
g_{11,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}
we proceed as for
g_{10,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}
and we focus on

\displaystyle g_{14,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})

\displaystyle=-8k^{3}\gamma_{0}(1-2\gamma_{0})\int_{I_{3}(y_{0})}\frac{h(z,y_{0})}{(v^{\prime}(z))^{2}}\left({\mathcal{G}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)_{\mathsf{s}}(y,y_{0},z)f_{\mathsf{s}}(z,y_{0})\xi^{-2-2\gamma_{0}}\mathrm{d}z

\displaystyle=\frac{4k^{2}\gamma_{0}(1-2\gamma_{0})}{1+2\gamma_{0}}\frac{h(z,y_{0})}{(v^{\prime}(z))^{2}}\left({\mathcal{G}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)_{\mathsf{s}}(y,y_{0},z)f_{\mathsf{s}}(z,y_{0})\xi^{-1-2\gamma_{0}}\Big|_{z\in\partial I_{3}(y_{0})}

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{4k^{2}\gamma_{0}(1-2\gamma_{0})}{1+2\gamma_{0}}\int_{I_{3}(y_{0})}\frac{h(z,y_{0})}{(v^{\prime}(z))^{2}}\partial_{z}\left(\left({\mathcal{G}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)_{\mathsf{s}}(y,y_{0},z)f_{\mathsf{s}}(z,y_{0})\right)\xi^{-1-2\gamma_{0}}\mathrm{d}z

\displaystyle\quad+k\gamma_{0}\frac{1-2\gamma_{0}}{1+2\gamma_{0}}\partial_{z}\left(\frac{h(z,y_{0})}{(v^{\prime}(z))^{2}}\right)\left({\mathcal{G}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)_{\mathsf{s}}(y,y_{0},z)f_{\mathsf{s}}(z,y_{0})\xi^{-2\gamma_{0}}\Big|_{z\in\partial I_{3}(y_{0})}

\displaystyle\quad-k\gamma_{0}\frac{1-2\gamma_{0}}{1+2\gamma_{0}}\int_{I_{3}(y_{0})}\partial_{z}\left(\left({\mathcal{G}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)_{\mathsf{s}}(y,y_{0},z)f_{\mathsf{s}}(z,y_{0})\partial_{z}\left(\frac{h(z,y_{0})}{(v^{\prime}(z))^{2}}\right)\right)\xi^{-2\gamma_{0}}

where we have integrated by parts twice. The vanishing and regularity of
h(\cdot,y_{0})
, Proposition
4.1
and Corollary
4.3
provide the
X_{k}^{1}
estimates for the boundary terms, of order 1 in
k
. The first integral is estimated with the same reasoning, combining the ideas of
g_{6,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}
and
g_{13,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}
, while for the second integral we have that

\displaystyle k(1-2\mu_{0})

\displaystyle\left\|\int_{I_{3}(y_{0})}\partial_{z}\left(\left({\mathcal{G}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)_{\mathsf{s}}(y,y_{0},z)f_{\mathsf{s}}(z,y_{0})\partial_{z}\left(\frac{h(z,y_{0})}{(v^{\prime}(z))^{2}}\right)\right)\xi^{-2\gamma_{0}}\mathrm{d}z\right\|_{X_{k}^{1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|f\|_{X_{k}^{1}}(1-2\mu_{0})\int_{0}^{3\beta}x^{-2\mu_{0}}\mathrm{d}x\lesssim\|f\|_{X_{k}},

we proceed as in
g_{13,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}
. With this, the proof is finished.
∎

As a corollary of Lemma
E.4
noting that

\displaystyle k^{2}\|h(\cdot,y_{0})\|_{L^{\infty}(I_{3}(y_{0}))}+k\|\partial_{y}h(\cdot,y_{0})\|_{L^{\infty}(I_{3}(y_{0}))}\lesssim 1.

for all
h(y,y_{0})\in C^{3}_{y}
uniformly in
y_{0}
with
h(y_{0},y_{0})=\partial_{y}h(y,y_{0})=0
, we have the following result.

Lemma E.5
.

Let
k\geq 1
and
y_{0}\in I_{S}\cup I_{W}
. Let
f(y,y_{0})\in X_{k}
and
h(y,y_{0})\in C^{3}_{y}
uniformly in
y_{0}
with
h(y_{0},y_{0})=\partial_{y}h(y,y_{0})=0
. Then,

\displaystyle\|R_{3,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}hf\|_{X_{k}}\lesssim k^{-1}\|f\|_{X_{k}},

uniformly for all
0<\varepsilon<\varepsilon_{*}
and all
y_{0}\in I_{S}\cup I_{W}
.

We finish the subsection studying how
R_{3,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}
acts on
H_{k}^{1}
functions weighted with a smooth quadratically vanishing pre-factor.

Lemma E.6
.

Let
k\geq 1
and
y_{0}\in I_{S}\cup I_{W}
. Let
f(y,y_{0})\in H_{k}^{1}
and
h(y,y_{0})\in C^{3}_{y}
uniformly in
y_{0}
with
h(y_{0},y_{0})=\partial_{y}h(y_{0},y_{0})=0
. Then,

\displaystyle\|R_{3,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}hf\|_{X_{k}}\lesssim\|f\|_{H_{k}^{1}},

uniformly for all
0<\varepsilon<\varepsilon_{*}
and all
y_{0}\in I_{S}\cup I_{W}
.

Proof.

We integrate by parts to have

\displaystyle g_{1,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}

\displaystyle:=\int_{I_{3}(y_{0})}{\mathcal{G}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0},z)\frac{h(z,y_{0})f(z,y_{0})}{(v(z)-v(y_{0})\pm i\varepsilon)^{3}}\mathrm{d}z

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{2}{\mathcal{G}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0},z)\frac{h(z,y_{0})f(z,y_{0})}{(v(z)-v(y_{0})\pm i\varepsilon)^{2}}\Big|_{z\in\partial I_{3}(y_{0})}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{2}\int_{I_{3}(y_{0})}\partial_{z}\left(\frac{h(z,y_{0})}{v^{\prime}(z)}\right){\mathcal{G}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0},z)\frac{f(z,y_{0})}{(v(z)-v(y_{0})\pm i\varepsilon)^{2}}\mathrm{d}z

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{2}\int_{I_{3}(y_{0})}\frac{h(z,y_{0})}{v^{\prime}(z)}{\mathcal{G}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0},z)\frac{\partial_{z}f(z,y_{0})}{(v(z)-v(y_{0})\pm i\varepsilon)^{2}}\mathrm{d}z

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{2}\int_{I_{3}(y_{0})}\frac{h(z,y_{0})}{v^{\prime}(z)}\frac{\partial_{z}{\mathcal{G}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0},z)}{(v(z)-v(y_{0})\pm i\varepsilon)^{2}}f(z,y_{0})\mathrm{d}z.

The quadratic vanishing of
h(\cdot,y_{0})
and Lemma
2.3
ensures

\displaystyle\left\|{\mathcal{G}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0},z)\frac{h(z,y_{0})f(z,y_{0})}{(v(z)-v(y_{0})\pm i\varepsilon)^{2}}\Big|_{z\in\partial I_{3}(y_{0})}\right\|_{X_{k}^{1}}\lesssim k^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|f\|_{H_{k}^{1}},

while we can argue as in Lemma
E.3
to bound

\displaystyle\left\|\int_{I_{3}(y_{0})}\partial_{z}\left(\frac{h(z,y_{0})}{v^{\prime}(z)}\right){\mathcal{G}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0},z)\frac{f(z,y_{0})}{(v(z)-v(y_{0})\pm i\varepsilon)^{2}}\mathrm{d}z\right\|_{X_{k}^{1}}\lesssim k^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|f\|_{H_{k}^{1}}.

Similarly, we can use the smooth quadratic vanishing of
h(\cdot,y_{0})
to conclude

\displaystyle\left\|\int_{I_{3}(y_{0})}\frac{h(z,y_{0})}{v^{\prime}(z)}{\mathcal{G}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0},z)\frac{\partial_{z}f(z,y_{0})}{(v(z)-v(y_{0})\pm i\varepsilon)^{2}}\mathrm{d}z\right\|_{X_{k}^{1}}\lesssim k^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|f\|_{H_{k}^{1}}.

Regarding
\partial_{z}{\mathcal{G}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(z,y_{0})
, we shall consider its most singular term, and we observe that

\displaystyle\left\|\int_{I_{3}(y_{0})}\frac{h(z,y_{0})}{v^{\prime}(z)}\frac{\partial_{z}\left({\mathcal{G}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)_{\mathsf{s}}(y,y_{0},z)\xi^{\frac{1}{2}-\gamma_{0}}+2k(\tfrac{1}{2}-\gamma_{0})\left({\mathcal{G}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)_{\mathsf{s}}(y,y_{0},z)\xi^{-\frac{1}{2}-\gamma_{0}}}{(v(z)-v(y_{0})\pm i\varepsilon)^{2}}f(z,y_{0})\mathrm{d}z\right\|_{X_{k}^{1}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\lesssim k^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|f\|_{H_{k}^{1}}

because of the smooth quadratic vanishing of
h(\cdot,y_{0})
, the embedding
\|f\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim k^{\frac{1}{2}}\|f\|_{H_{k}^{1}}
coming from Lemma
2.3
, Propositions
4.1
,
4.2
and Corollary
4.3
. This completes the proof.
∎

E.3.
Fourth order operator estimates

We next define the most singular operator

\displaystyle R_{4,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}f(y,y_{0})=\int_{0}^{2}{\mathcal{G}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0},z)\frac{f(z)}{(v(z)-v(y_{0})\pm i\varepsilon)^{4}}\mathrm{d}z.

The next result shows that
R_{4,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}
maps
X_{k}
functions weighted with smooth quadratically vanishing pre-factors into
X_{k}
.

Lemma E.7
.

Let
k\geq 1
and
y_{0}\in I_{S}\cup I_{W}
. Let
f(y,y_{0})\in X_{k}
and
h(y,y_{0})\in C^{3}_{y}
uniformly in
y_{0}
with
h(y_{0},y_{0})=\partial_{y}h(y_{0},y_{0})=0
. Then,

\displaystyle\|R_{4,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}hf\|_{X_{k}}\lesssim\|f\|_{X_{k}},

uniformly for all
0<\varepsilon<\varepsilon_{*}
and all
y_{0}\in I_{S}\cup I_{W}
.

Proof.

For
g_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0}):=R_{4,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}hf(y,y_{0})
we write

\displaystyle g_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})

\displaystyle=\int_{I_{3}(y_{0})}{\mathcal{G}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0},z)\frac{h(z,y_{0})f(z,y_{0})}{(v(z)-v(y_{0})\pm i\varepsilon)^{4}}\mathrm{d}z+\int_{I_{3}^{c}(y_{0})}{\mathcal{G}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0},z)\frac{h(z,y_{0})f(z,y_{0})}{(v(z)-v(y_{0})\pm i\varepsilon)^{4}}\mathrm{d}z

\displaystyle=g_{1,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})+g_{2,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0}).

As usual, we have
\|g_{2,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\|_{X_{k}^{1}}\lesssim\|f\|_{X_{k}}
. For the local contribution, we integrate by parts once,

\displaystyle g_{1,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{3}\frac{h(z,y_{0})}{v^{\prime}(z)}\frac{{\mathcal{G}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0},z)f(z,y_{0})}{(v(z)-v(y_{0})\pm i\varepsilon)^{3}}\Big|_{z\in\partial I_{3}(y_{0})}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{3}\int_{I_{3}(y_{0})}\partial_{z}\left(\frac{h(z,y_{0})}{v^{\prime}(z)}\right)\frac{{\mathcal{G}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0},z)f(z,y_{0})}{(v(z)-v(y_{0})\pm i\varepsilon)^{3}}\mathrm{d}z

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{3}\int_{I_{3}(y_{0})}\frac{h(z,y_{0})}{v^{\prime}(z)}\frac{\partial_{z}\left({\mathcal{G}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0},z)f(z,y_{0})\right)}{(v(z)-v(y_{0})\pm i\varepsilon)^{3}}\mathrm{d}z.

Proposition
4.1
, Corollayr
4.3
and the smooth quadratic vanishing of
h(\cdot,y_{0})
yield

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{h(z,y_{0})}{v^{\prime}(z)}\frac{{\mathcal{G}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0},z)f(z,y_{0})}{(v(z)-v(y_{0})\pm i\varepsilon)^{3}}\Big|_{z\in\partial I_{3}(y_{0})}\right\|_{X_{k}^{1}}\lesssim\|f\|_{X_{k}^{1}},

while the arguments of Lemma
E.4
show that

\displaystyle\left\|\int_{I_{3}(y_{0})}\partial_{z}\left(\frac{h(z,y_{0})}{v^{\prime}(z)}\right)\frac{{\mathcal{G}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0},z)f(z,y_{0})}{(v(z)-v(y_{0})\pm i\varepsilon)^{3}}\mathrm{d}z\right\|_{X_{k}^{1}}\lesssim\|f\|_{X_{k}^{1}}.

Next, since
f\in X_{k}
, from (
6.4
) we consider the most singular contributions of
\partial_{z}\left({\mathcal{G}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0},z)f(z,y_{0})\right)
, namely

\displaystyle g_{3,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})

\displaystyle:=\frac{1}{3}\int_{I_{3}(y_{0})}\frac{h(z,y_{0})}{v^{\prime}(z)}\frac{\partial_{z}\left(\left({\mathcal{G}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)_{\mathsf{s}}(y,y_{0},z)f_{\mathsf{s}}(z,y_{0})\right)}{(v(z)-v(y_{0})\pm i\varepsilon)^{3}}\xi^{1-2\gamma_{0}}\mathrm{d}z,

\displaystyle g_{4,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})

\displaystyle:=\frac{2k(1-2\gamma_{0})}{3}\int_{I_{3}(y_{0})}\frac{h(z,y_{0})}{v^{\prime}(z)}\frac{\left({\mathcal{G}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)_{\mathsf{s}}(y,y_{0},z)f_{\mathsf{s}}(z,y_{0})}{(v(z)-v(y_{0})\pm i\varepsilon)^{3}}\xi^{-2\gamma_{0}}\mathrm{d}z.

For
g_{3,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}
, thanks to the vanishing of
\partial_{z}\left(\left({\mathcal{G}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)_{\mathsf{s}}(y,y_{0},z)f_{\mathsf{s}}(z,y_{0})\right)
coming from Proposition
4.2
, Corollary
4.3
and
f\in X_{k}^{1}
, and the smooth quadratic vanishing of
h(\cdot,y_{0})
and we argue as in
g_{6,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}
in the proof of Lemma
E.4
to obtain
\|g_{3,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\|_{X_{k}^{1}}\lesssim\|f\|_{X_{k}^{1}}
. We now address
g_{4,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}
. Since

\displaystyle\frac{1}{v(y)-v(y_{0})\pm i\varepsilon}=\frac{(v^{\prime}(y_{0}))^{-1}}{y-y_{0}\pm i\varepsilon_{0}}-\mathrm{V}_{1,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})

with
\mathrm{V}_{1,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})\in L^{\infty}(0,2)
uniformly for all
y_{0}\in[0,2]
and all
0<\varepsilon
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{(v(y)-v(y_{0})\pm i\varepsilon)^{3}}=\frac{(v^{\prime}(y_{0}))^{-3}}{(y-y_{0}\pm i\varepsilon_{0})^{3}}+\frac{1}{(y-y_{0}\pm i\varepsilon)^{2}}\mathrm{V}_{3,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0}),

with
\mathrm{V}_{3,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})\in L^{\infty}(0,2)
uniformly for all
y_{0}\in[0,2]
and all
0<\varepsilon
. Therefore,

\displaystyle g_{4,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})

\displaystyle=(2k)^{4}\frac{1-2\gamma_{0}}{3(v^{\prime}(y_{0}))^{3}}\int_{I_{3}(y_{0})}\frac{h(z,y_{0})}{v^{\prime}(z)}\left({\mathcal{G}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)_{\mathsf{s}}(y,y_{0},z)f_{\mathsf{s}}(z,y_{0})\xi^{-3-2\gamma_{0}}\mathrm{d}z

\displaystyle\quad+(2k)^{3}\frac{1-2\gamma_{0}}{3(v^{\prime}(y_{0}))^{2}}\int_{I_{3}(y_{0})}\frac{h(z,y_{0})}{v^{\prime}(z)}\left({\mathcal{G}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)_{\mathsf{s}}(y,y_{0},z)f_{\mathsf{s}}(z,y_{0})\mathrm{V}_{3,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(z,y_{0})\xi^{-2-2\gamma_{0}}\mathrm{d}z

\displaystyle=g_{5,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})+g_{6,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0}).

The quadratic vanishing of
h(\cdot,y_{0})
,
(1-2\mu_{0})\int_{0}^{3\beta}x^{-2\mu_{0}}\mathrm{d}x\lesssim 1
and Proposition
4.1
provide the estimate
\left\|g_{6,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right\|_{X_{k}^{1}}\lesssim\|f\|_{X_{k}^{1}}
. Integrating by parts whenever
\partial_{z}
does not land on

\mathrm{f}_{{\mathsf{s}},k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0},z):=\left({\mathcal{G}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)_{\mathsf{s}}(y,y_{0},z)f_{\mathsf{s}}(z,y_{0}),

we have

\displaystyle g_{5,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})

\displaystyle=-\frac{(2k)^{3}}{3(v^{\prime}(y_{0}))^{3}}\frac{1-2\gamma_{0}}{2+2\gamma_{0}}\frac{h(z,y_{0})}{v^{\prime}(z)}\mathrm{f}_{{\mathsf{s}},k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0},z)\xi^{-2-2\gamma_{0}}\Big|_{z\in\partial I_{3}(y_{0})}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{(2k)^{3}}{3(v^{\prime}(y_{0}))^{3}}\frac{1-2\gamma_{0}}{2+2\gamma_{0}}\int_{I_{3}(y_{0})}\frac{h(z,y_{0})}{v^{\prime}(z)}\partial_{z}\mathrm{f}_{{\mathsf{s}},k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0},z)\xi^{-2-2\gamma_{0}}\mathrm{d}z

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{(2k)^{2}}{3(v^{\prime}(y_{0}))^{3}}\frac{1-2\gamma_{0}}{(2+2\gamma_{0})(1+2\gamma_{0})}\partial_{z}\left(\frac{h(z,y_{0})}{v^{\prime}(z)}\right)\mathrm{f}_{{\mathsf{s}},k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0},z)\xi^{-1-2\gamma_{0}}\Big|_{z\in\partial I_{3}(y_{0})}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{(2k)^{2}}{3(v^{\prime}(y_{0}))^{3}}\frac{1-2\gamma_{0}}{(2+2\gamma_{0})(1+2\gamma_{0})}\int_{I_{3}(y_{0})}\partial_{z}\left(\frac{h(z,y_{0})}{v^{\prime}(z)}\right)\mathrm{f}_{{\mathsf{s}},k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0},z)\xi^{-1-2\gamma_{0}}\mathrm{d}z

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{2k}{3(v^{\prime}(y_{0}))^{3}}\frac{1-2\gamma_{0}}{(2+2\gamma_{0})(1+2\gamma_{0})2\gamma_{0}}\partial_{z}^{2}\left(\frac{h(z,y_{0})}{v^{\prime}(z)}\right)\mathrm{f}_{{\mathsf{s}},k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0},z)\xi^{-2\gamma_{0}}\Big|_{z\in\partial I_{3}(y_{0})}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{2k}{3(v^{\prime}(y_{0}))^{3}}\frac{1-2\gamma_{0}}{(2+2\gamma_{0})(1+2\gamma_{0})2\gamma_{0}}\int_{I_{3}(y_{0})}\partial_{z}\left(\partial_{z}^{2}\left(\frac{h(z,y_{0})}{v^{\prime}(z)}\right)\mathrm{f}_{{\mathsf{s}},k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0},z)\right)\xi^{-2\gamma_{0}}\mathrm{d}z.

The application of Proposition
4.1
, Propistion
4.2
and Corollary
4.3
, together with the smooth quadratic vanishing of
h(\cdot,y_{0})
and the observation that
(1-2\mu_{0})\int_{0}^{3\beta}x^{-2\mu_{0}}\mathrm{d}x\lesssim 1
give the desired bounds, we omit the routine details.
∎

Appendix F
High-order operator estimates for mild stratifications

In this subsection we address the analogue results of Section
E
for the mildly stratified regime
y_{0}\in I_{M}
.

F.1.
Second order operator estimates

We begin the section showing the mapping properties of
R_{2,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}
.

Lemma F.1
.

Let
k\geq 1
,
y_{0}\in I_{M}
and
f(y,y_{0})\in LX_{k}
. There holds

\displaystyle\|(R_{2,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}f)(\cdot,y_{0})\|_{LX_{k}}\lesssim\|f\|_{LX_{k}}

uniformly in
0<\varepsilon<\varepsilon_{*}
, and in
y_{0}\in I_{M}
.

Proof.

Let
g_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})=R_{2,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}f(y,y_{0})
. We have

\displaystyle g_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})

\displaystyle=\frac{(2k)^{2}}{(v^{\prime}(y_{0}))^{2}}\int_{I_{3}(y_{0})}{\mathcal{G}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0},z)\frac{f(z,y_{0})}{\xi^{2}}\mathrm{d}z+\int_{0}^{2}{\mathcal{G}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0},z)\frac{\mathrm{V}_{2,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(z,y_{0})f(z,y_{0})}{v(z)-v(y_{0})\pm i\varepsilon}\mathrm{d}z

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{(2k)^{2}}{(v^{\prime}(y_{0}))^{2}}\int_{I_{3}^{c}(y_{0})}{\mathcal{G}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0},z)\frac{f(z,y_{0})}{\xi^{2}}\mathrm{d}z

\displaystyle=g_{1,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})+g_{2,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})+g_{3,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0}),

where we have used that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{(v(z)-v(y_{0})\pm i\varepsilon)^{2}}=\frac{(v^{\prime}(y_{0}))^{-2}}{(y-y_{0}\pm i\varepsilon_{0})^{2}}+\frac{\mathrm{V}_{2,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})}{v(y)-v(y_{0})\pm i\varepsilon},

with
\mathrm{V}_{2,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(\cdot,y_{0})\in L^{\infty}
uniformly in
y_{0}\in(0,2)
and in
0<\varepsilon<\varepsilon_{*}
. Hence, we can use Lemma
6.8
to estimate
g_{2,k,\varepsilon}
. The bounds for
g_{3,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}
are deduced from Proposition
5.1
and Corollary
5.3
. Using the regularity structures of
{\mathcal{G}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}
and of
f\in LX_{k}
, we have

\displaystyle(v^{\prime}(y_{0}))^{2}g_{1,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})=g_{4,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})+g_{5,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})+g_{6,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0}),

with

\displaystyle g_{4,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})

\displaystyle=(2k)^{2}\int_{I_{3}(y_{0})}\left({\mathcal{G}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)_{\mathsf{r}}f_{\mathsf{r}}(z,y_{0})\xi^{-1+2\gamma_{0}}\mathrm{d}z,

\displaystyle g_{5,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})

\displaystyle=(2k)^{2}\int_{I_{3}(y_{0})}\left(\left({\mathcal{G}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)_{\mathsf{r}}f_{\mathsf{s}}(z,y_{0})+\left({\mathcal{G}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)_{\mathsf{s}}f_{\mathsf{r}}(z,y_{0})\right)\xi^{-1}\log(\xi){\mathcal{Q}}_{\gamma_{0}}(\xi)\mathrm{d}z,

\displaystyle g_{6,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})

\displaystyle=(2k)^{2}\int_{I_{3}(y_{0})}\left({\mathcal{G}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)_{\mathsf{s}}f_{\mathsf{s}}(z,y_{0})\xi^{-1-2\gamma_{0}}\left(\log(\xi){\mathcal{Q}}_{\gamma_{0}}(\xi)\right)^{2}\mathrm{d}z.

To bound the three contributions, we recall Lemma
A.1
, namely

\displaystyle\frac{\xi^{2\gamma_{0}}-1}{2\gamma_{0}}=\log(\xi){\mathcal{Q}}_{\gamma_{0}}(\xi),

with
|{\mathcal{Q}}_{\gamma_{0}}(\xi)|\lesssim 1
for
|\xi|
bounded. To estimate
g_{4,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}
in
LX_{k}^{1}
, we note that

\displaystyle g_{4,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})

\displaystyle=2k\left({\mathcal{G}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)_{\mathsf{r}}f_{\mathsf{r}}(z,y_{0})\log(\xi){\mathcal{Q}}_{\gamma_{0}}(\xi)\Big|_{z\in\partial I_{3}(y_{0})}

\displaystyle\quad-2k\int_{I_{3}(y_{0})}\partial_{z}\left(\left({\mathcal{G}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)_{\mathsf{r}}f_{\mathsf{r}}(z,y_{0})\right)\log(\xi){\mathcal{Q}}_{\gamma_{0}}(\xi)\mathrm{d}z

and the bounds follow from Propositions
5.1
,
5.2
and Corollary
5.3
. Next, for
g_{5,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}
we note that for

{\mathcal{Q}}_{2,\gamma_{0}}(\xi):=\int_{0}^{1}\frac{e^{2\gamma_{0}u\log(\xi)}-1}{2\gamma_{0}u}\mathrm{d}u

(F.1)

we have
2k\zeta^{-1}{\mathcal{Q}}_{\gamma_{0}}(\zeta)=\partial_{z}{\mathcal{Q}}_{2,\gamma_{0}}(\xi)
. Likewise, for

{\mathcal{Q}}_{3,\gamma_{0}}(\xi):=\int_{0}^{1}\left(\frac{e^{2\gamma_{0}u\log(\xi)}-1}{2\gamma_{0}u}\right)^{2}\mathrm{d}u

(F.2)

we also have
2k\zeta^{-1}{\mathcal{Q}}_{2,\gamma_{0}}(\xi)=\frac{1}{2}\partial_{z}{\mathcal{Q}}_{3,\gamma_{0}}(\xi)
. Furthermore,

\displaystyle\left|{\mathcal{Q}}_{j,\gamma_{0}}(\xi)\right|\lesssim|\log(\xi)|^{j-1},

for
j=2,3
. Therefore, for
\mathrm{f}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0},z):=\left({\mathcal{G}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)_{\mathsf{r}}f_{\mathsf{s}}(z,y_{0})+\left({\mathcal{G}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)_{\mathsf{s}}f_{\mathsf{r}}(z,y_{0})
, we have

\displaystyle g_{5,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}

\displaystyle=2k\,\mathrm{f}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0},z)\log(\xi){\mathcal{Q}}_{2,\gamma_{0}}(\xi)\Big|_{z\in\partial I_{3}(y_{0})}-2k\int_{I_{3}(y_{0})}\partial_{z}\mathrm{f}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0},z)\log(\xi){\mathcal{Q}}_{2,\gamma_{0}}(\xi)\mathrm{d}z

\displaystyle\quad-\mathrm{f}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0},z){\mathcal{Q}}_{3,\gamma_{0}}(\xi)\Big|_{z\in\partial I_{3}(y_{0})}+\int_{I_{3}(y_{0})}\partial_{z}\mathrm{f}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0},z){\mathcal{Q}}_{3,\gamma_{0}}(\xi)\mathrm{d}z.

Then, the
LX_{k}^{1}
estimates are obtained as usual from Propositions
5.1
,
5.2
and Corollary
5.3
. Finally, for
g_{6,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}
we first note from Lemma
A.2
that

\displaystyle 2k\xi^{-1-2\gamma_{0}}\left(\log(\xi){\mathcal{Q}}_{\gamma_{0}}(\xi)\right)^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{2k}{4\gamma_{0}^{2}}\left(\xi^{-1+2\gamma_{0}}-2\xi^{-1}+\xi^{-1-2\gamma_{0}}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{4\gamma_{0}^{2}}\partial_{z}\left(\frac{\xi^{2\gamma_{0}}-1}{2\gamma_{0}}-2\log(\xi)+\frac{1-\xi^{-2\gamma_{0}}}{2\gamma_{0}}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{4\gamma_{0}^{2}}\partial_{z}\mathrm{Q}_{\gamma_{0}}(\xi)

Thus, integrating by parts we reach

\displaystyle g_{6,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})

\displaystyle=\frac{2k}{4\gamma_{0}^{2}}\left({\mathcal{G}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)_{\mathsf{s}}f_{\mathsf{s}}(z,y_{0})\mathrm{Q}_{\gamma_{0}}(\xi)\Big|_{z\in\partial I_{3}(y_{0})}

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{2k}{4\gamma_{0}^{2}}\int_{I_{3}(y_{0})}\partial_{z}\left(\left({\mathcal{G}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)_{\mathsf{s}}f_{\mathsf{s}}(z,y_{0})\right)\mathrm{Q}_{\gamma_{0}}(\xi)\mathrm{d}z.

Together with Lemma
A.2
, Propositions
5.1
,
5.2
and Corollary
5.3
, we obtain the estimates for
g_{6,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}
and the proof is finished.
∎

We can combine Lemma
F.1
and Lemma
6.8
to obtain the following

Corollary F.2
.

Let
k\geq 1
and
y_{0}\in I_{S}M
. Let
f\in X_{k}
and
h(z,y_{0})\in C^{2}
uniformly in
y_{0}
. Then,

\displaystyle\|R_{2,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}hf\|_{X_{k}}\lesssim\|f\|_{X_{k}},

uniformly for all
0<\varepsilon<\varepsilon_{*}
and all
y_{0}\in I_{M}
.

Proof.

It follows from Lemma
F.1
and Lemma
6.8
once we write

\displaystyle g_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})=R_{2,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}hf(y,y_{0})=h(y_{0})R_{2,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}f(y,y_{0})+\left(R_{1,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\frac{h_{1}(\cdot,y_{0})}{v(\cdot)-v(y_{0})\pm i\varepsilon}f(\cdot,y_{0})\right)(y),

where we have defined
h_{1}(y,y_{0})=h(y,y_{0})-h(y_{0},y_{0})
, with
h_{1}(y_{0},y_{0})=0
and
h_{1}(\cdot,y_{0})\in C^{2}
uniformly in
y_{0}\in[0,2]
.
∎

F.2.
Third order operator estimates

We next address the mapping properties of
R_{3,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}
in the regime
y_{0}\in I_{M}
.

Lemma F.3
.

Let
k\geq 1
,
y_{0}\in I_{M}
and
f(y,y_{0})\in LX_{k}
. Let
h(y,y_{0})
be such that
h(y_{0},y_{0})=0
and
h(\cdot,y_{0})\in C^{3}
uniformly in
y_{0}
. There holds

\displaystyle\|(R_{3,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}hf)(\cdot,y_{0})\|_{LX_{k}}\lesssim\|f\|_{LX_{k}}

uniformly in
0<\varepsilon<\varepsilon_{*}
, and in
y_{0}\in I_{M}
.

Proof.

We argue as in the proof of Lemma
F.1
, we shall focus on

\displaystyle g_{1,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}

\displaystyle=(2k)^{3}\int_{I_{3}(y_{0})}{\mathcal{G}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0},z)h(z,y_{0})f(z,y_{0})\xi^{-3}\mathrm{d}z

\displaystyle=(2k)^{3}\int_{I_{3}(y_{0})}\left({\mathcal{G}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)_{\mathsf{r}}(y,y_{0},z)h(z,y_{0})f_{\mathsf{r}}(z,y_{0})\xi^{-2+2\gamma_{0}}\mathrm{d}z

\displaystyle\quad+(2k)^{3}\int_{I_{3}(y_{0})}\mathrm{f}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0},z)h(z,y_{0})\xi^{-2}\frac{\xi^{2\gamma_{0}}-1}{2\gamma_{0}}\mathrm{d}z

\displaystyle\quad+(2k)^{3}\int_{I_{3}(y_{0})}\left({\mathcal{G}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)_{\mathsf{s}}(y,y_{0},z)h(z,y_{0})f_{\mathsf{s}}(z,y_{0})\xi^{-2-2\gamma_{0}}\left(\frac{\xi^{2\gamma_{0}}-1}{2\gamma_{0}}\right)^{2}\mathrm{d}z

\displaystyle=g_{2,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})+g_{3,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})+g_{4,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0}).

Integrating by parts, we have

\displaystyle g_{2,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})

\displaystyle=-\frac{(2k)^{2}}{1-2\gamma_{0}}\left({\mathcal{G}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)_{\mathsf{r}}(y,y_{0},z)h(z,y_{0})f_{\mathsf{r}}(z,y_{0})\xi^{-1+2\gamma_{0}}\Big|_{z\in\partial I_{3}(y_{0}}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{(2k)^{2}}{1-2\gamma_{0}}\int_{I_{3}(y_{0})}h(z,y_{0})\partial_{z}\left(\left({\mathcal{G}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)_{\mathsf{r}}(y,y_{0},z)f_{\mathsf{r}}(z,y_{0})\right)\xi^{-1+2\gamma_{0}}\mathrm{d}z

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{2k}{1-2\gamma_{0}}\partial_{z}h(z,y_{0})\left({\mathcal{G}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)_{\mathsf{r}}(y,y_{0},z)f_{\mathsf{r}}(z,y_{0})\log(\xi){\mathcal{Q}}_{\gamma_{0}}(\xi)\Big|_{z\in\partial I_{3}(y_{0})}

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{2k}{1-2\gamma_{0}}\int_{I_{3}(y_{0})}\partial_{z}\left(\partial_{z}h(z,y_{0})\left({\mathcal{G}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)_{\mathsf{r}}(y,y_{0},z)f_{\mathsf{r}}(z,y_{0})\right)\log(\xi){\mathcal{Q}}_{\gamma_{0}}(\xi)\mathrm{d}z

and the bounds for
g_{2,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}
follow from the usual applications of Propositions
5.1
,
5.2
and Corollary
5.3
. For
g_{3,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}
, we have

\displaystyle g_{3,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})

\displaystyle=(2k)^{2}\mathrm{f}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0},z)h(z,y_{0})\frac{\xi^{-1}}{2\gamma_{0}-1}\left(1+\log(\xi){\mathcal{Q}}_{\gamma_{0}}(\xi)\right)\Big|_{z\in\partial I_{3}(y_{0})}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{(2k)^{2}}{1-2\gamma_{0}}\int_{I_{3}(y_{0})}\partial_{z}\left(\mathrm{f}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0},z)h(z,y_{0})\right)\xi^{-1}(1+\log(\xi){\mathcal{Q}}_{\gamma_{0}}(\xi))\mathrm{d}z

and thus we see that the estimates follow from the arguments in the proof of Lemma
F.1
for the term involving
\mathrm{f}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0},z)\partial_{z}h(z,y_{0})
and from the vanishing of
h(z,y_{0})
at
z=y_{0}
for the contribution containing
\partial_{z}\mathrm{f}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0},z)h(z,y_{0})
, we omit the details. Finally, for
g_{4,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}
we observe that

\displaystyle 2k\xi^{-2-2\gamma_{0}}\left(\frac{\xi^{2\gamma_{0}}-1}{2\gamma_{0}}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{4\gamma_{0}^{2}}\partial_{z}\left(\frac{\xi^{-1+2\gamma_{0}}}{-1+2\gamma_{0}}+2\xi^{-1}-\frac{\xi^{-1-2\gamma_{0}}}{1+2\gamma_{0}}\right),

with

\displaystyle 2k

\displaystyle\left(\frac{\xi^{-1+2\gamma_{0}}}{-1+2\gamma_{0}}+2\xi^{-1}-\frac{\xi^{-1-2\gamma_{0}}}{1+2\gamma_{0}}\right)

(F.3)

\displaystyle=\xi^{-1}\frac{2k}{4\gamma_{0}^{2}-1}\left(2\gamma_{0}\left(\xi^{2\gamma_{0}}-\xi^{-2\gamma_{0}}\right)+\xi^{-2\gamma_{0}}\left(\xi^{2\gamma_{0}}-1\right)^{2}+8\gamma_{0}^{2}\right)

(F.4)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{4\gamma_{0}^{2}-1}\partial_{z}\left((1+2\gamma_{0})\frac{\xi^{2\gamma_{0}}-1}{2\gamma_{0}}+2(4\gamma_{0}^{2}-1)\log(\xi)-(2\gamma_{0}-1)\frac{1-\xi^{-2\gamma_{0}}}{2\gamma_{0}}\right).

(F.5)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{4\gamma_{0}^{2}-1}\partial_{z}\left(\mathrm{Q}_{\gamma_{0}}(\xi)+\xi^{-2\gamma_{0}}\left(\xi^{2\gamma_{0}}-1\right)^{2}+8\gamma_{0}^{2}\log(\xi)\right)

(F.6)

Hence, for
\mathrm{f}_{{\mathsf{s}},k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0},z):=\left({\mathcal{G}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)_{\mathsf{s}}(y,y_{0},z)f_{\mathsf{s}}(z,y_{0})
, we have

\displaystyle g_{4,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})

\displaystyle=\frac{(2k)^{2}}{4\gamma_{0}^{2}}\mathrm{f}_{{\mathsf{s}},k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0},z)h(z,y_{0})\left(\frac{\xi^{-1+2\gamma_{0}}}{-1+2\gamma_{0}}+2\xi^{-1}-\frac{\xi^{-1-2\gamma_{0}}}{1+2\gamma_{0}}\right)\Big|_{z\in\partial I_{3}(y_{0})}

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{(2k)^{2}}{4\gamma_{0}^{2}}\int_{I_{3}(y_{0})}\frac{h(z,y_{0})}{4\gamma_{0}^{2}-1}\partial_{z}\mathrm{f}_{{\mathsf{s}},k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0},z)\left(\frac{\xi^{-1+2\gamma_{0}}}{-1+2\gamma_{0}}+2\xi^{-1}-\frac{\xi^{-1-2\gamma_{0}}}{1+2\gamma_{0}}\right)\mathrm{d}z

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{2k}{4\gamma_{0}^{2}}\frac{\partial_{z}h(z,y_{0})}{4\gamma_{0}^{2}-1}\mathrm{f}_{{\mathsf{s}},k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0},z)\left(\mathrm{Q}_{\gamma_{0}}(\xi)+\xi^{-2\gamma_{0}}\left(\xi^{2\gamma_{0}}-1\right)^{2}+8\gamma_{0}^{2}\log(\xi)\right)\Big|_{z\in\partial I_{3}(y_{0})}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{2k}{4\gamma_{0}^{2}}\int_{I_{3}(y_{0})}\partial_{z}\left(\frac{\partial_{z}h(z,y_{0})}{4\gamma_{0}^{2}-1}\mathrm{f}_{{\mathsf{s}},k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0},z)\right)\left(\mathrm{Q}_{\gamma_{0}}(\xi)+\xi^{-2\gamma_{0}}\left(\xi^{2\gamma_{0}}-1\right)^{2}+8\gamma_{0}^{2}\log(\xi)\right)\mathrm{d}z

and the
LX_{k}^{1}
estimates for
g_{4,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}
follow from (
F.4
), (
F.6
), the observations that

\displaystyle\left|2\gamma_{0}\left(\xi^{2\gamma_{0}}-\xi^{-2\gamma_{0}}\right)+\xi^{-2\gamma_{0}}\left(\xi^{2\gamma_{0}}-1\right)^{2}+8\gamma_{0}^{2}\right|\lesssim\gamma_{0}^{2}\left(1+|\log(\xi)|^{3}\right)

and

\displaystyle\left|\mathrm{Q}_{\gamma_{0}}(\xi)+\xi^{-2\gamma_{0}}\left(\xi^{2\gamma_{0}}-1\right)^{2}+8\gamma_{0}^{2}\log(\xi)\right|\lesssim|\xi|^{-\frac{1}{2}}\gamma_{0}^{2}\left(1+|\log(\xi)|\right)

and the usual usage of Propositions
5.1
,
5.2
and Corollary
5.3
.
∎

F.3.
Fourth order operator estimates

We finish the section addressing the bounds for the most singular operator
R_{4,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}
in the mildly stratified regime.

Lemma F.4
.

Let
k\geq 1
and
y_{0}\in I_{M}
. Let
f(y,y_{0})\in X_{k}
and
h(y,y_{0})\in C^{3}_{y}
uniformly in
y_{0}
with
h(y_{0},y_{0})=\partial_{y}h(y_{0},y_{0})=0
. Then,

\displaystyle\|R_{4,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}hf\|_{LX_{k}}\lesssim\|f\|_{LX_{k}},

uniformly for all
0<\varepsilon<\varepsilon_{*}
and all
y_{0}\in I_{M}
.

Proof.

We shall argue as in the proofs of Lemma
F.1
and Lemma
F.3
, writing

\displaystyle g_{1,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})=g_{2,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})+g_{3,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})+g_{4,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0}),

where now

\displaystyle g_{2,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})

\displaystyle=(2k)^{4}\int_{I_{3}(y_{0})}h(z,y_{0})\mathrm{f}_{{\mathsf{r}},k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(z,y_{0},y)\xi^{-3+2\gamma_{0}}\mathrm{d}z,

\displaystyle g_{3,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})

\displaystyle=(2k)^{4}\int_{I_{3}(y_{0})}h(z,y_{0})\mathrm{f}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(z,y_{0},y)\xi^{-3}\frac{\xi^{2\gamma_{0}}-1}{2\gamma_{0}}\mathrm{d}z,

\displaystyle g_{3,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})

\displaystyle=(2k)^{4}\int_{I_{3}(y_{0})}h(z,y_{0})\mathrm{f}_{{\mathsf{s}},k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(z,y_{0},y)\xi^{-3-2\gamma_{0}}\left(\frac{\xi^{2\gamma_{0}}-1}{2\gamma_{0}}\right)^{2}\mathrm{d}z.

For
g_{2,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}
we note that

\displaystyle(2k)^{3}\xi^{-3+2\gamma_{0}}=\frac{(2k)^{2}}{2\gamma_{0}-1}\partial_{z}\xi^{-2+2\gamma_{0}}=\frac{2k}{(2\gamma_{0}-2)(2\gamma_{0}-1)}\partial_{z}^{2}\xi^{-1+2\gamma_{0}}.

Hence,

\displaystyle g_{2,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})

\displaystyle=\frac{(2k)^{3}}{2\gamma_{0}-2}h(z,y_{0})\mathrm{f}_{{\mathsf{r}},k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0},z)\xi^{-2+2\gamma_{0}}\Big|_{z\in\partial I_{3}(y_{0})}

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{(2k)^{3}}{2\gamma_{0}-2}\int_{I_{3}(y_{0})}h(z,y_{0})\partial_{z}\mathrm{f}_{{\mathsf{r}},k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0},z)\xi^{-2+2\gamma_{0}}\mathrm{d}z

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{(2k)^{2}}{(2\gamma_{0}-2)(2\gamma_{0}-1)}\partial_{z}h(z,y_{0})\mathrm{f}_{{\mathsf{r}},k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0},z)\xi^{-1+2\gamma_{0}}\Big|_{z\in\partial I_{3}(y_{0})}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{(2k)^{2}}{(2\gamma_{0}-2)(2\gamma_{0}-1)}\int_{I_{3}(y_{0})}\partial_{z}\left(\partial_{z}h(z,y_{0})\mathrm{f}_{{\mathsf{r}},k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0},z)\right)\xi^{-1+2\gamma_{0}}\mathrm{d}z,

from which the
LX_{k}^{1}
estimates follow, after using the smooth quadratic vanishing properties of
h(z,y_{0})
at
z=y_{0}
. For
g_{3,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}
, we now argue

\displaystyle 2k\xi^{-3}\left(\xi^{2\gamma_{0}}-1\right)=\partial_{z}\left(\frac{\xi^{2\gamma_{0}-2}}{-2+2\gamma_{0}}+\frac{\xi^{-2}}{2}\right)=\partial_{z}\left(\frac{\xi^{-2}}{2\gamma_{0}-2}\left(\xi^{2\gamma_{0}}-1+\gamma_{0}\right)\right)

with further

\displaystyle 2k\left(\frac{\xi^{2\gamma_{0}-2}}{-2+2\gamma_{0}}+\frac{\xi^{-2}}{2}\right)

\displaystyle=\partial_{z}\left(\frac{\xi^{2\gamma_{0}-1}}{(2\gamma_{0}-2)(2\gamma_{0}-1)}-\frac{\xi^{-1}}{2}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{\xi^{-1}}{(2\gamma_{0}-2)(2\gamma_{0}-1)}\left(\xi^{2\gamma_{0}}-1+2\gamma_{0}^{2}-3\gamma_{0}\right)

and finally

\displaystyle 2k\left(\frac{\xi^{2\gamma_{0}-1}}{(2\gamma_{0}-2)(2\gamma_{0}-1)}-\frac{\xi^{-1}}{2}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{(2\gamma_{0}-2)(2\gamma_{0}-1)}\partial_{z}\left(\frac{\xi^{2\gamma_{0}}-1}{2\gamma_{0}}-(\gamma_{0}-1)(2\gamma_{0}-1)\log(\xi)\right)

\displaystyle\quad=\frac{1}{4\gamma_{0}^{2}-6\gamma_{0}+2}\left(\frac{\xi^{2\gamma_{0}}-1}{2\gamma_{0}}-\log(\xi)-(2\gamma_{0}^{2}-3\gamma_{0})\log(\xi)\right)

so that a repeated integration by parts on the integral terms of the form
\mathrm{f}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0},z)\partial_{z}^{n}h(z,y_{0})
for
n\geq 0
, together with the quadratic vanishing of
h(z,y_{0})
at
z=y_{0}
and the estimates of
\partial_{z}^{n}\mathrm{f}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}
for
n=0,1
yields the desired result, we omit the details. Finally, for
g_{4,k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}
we observe that

\displaystyle(2k)\left(\xi^{-3+2\gamma_{0}}-2\xi^{-3}+\xi^{-3+2\gamma_{0}}\right)

\displaystyle=\partial_{z}\left(\frac{\xi^{-2+2\gamma_{0}}}{2\gamma_{0}-2}+\xi^{-2}-\frac{\xi^{-2-2\gamma_{0}}}{2\gamma_{0}+2}\right)

\displaystyle=\partial_{z}\left(\frac{\xi^{-2}}{4\gamma_{0}^{2}-4}\left(2\gamma_{0}\left(\xi^{2\gamma_{0}}-\xi^{-2\gamma_{0}}\right)+2\xi^{-2\gamma_{0}}\left(\xi^{2\gamma_{0}}-1\right)^{2}+4\gamma_{0}^{2}\right)\right)

with further

\displaystyle 2k\left(\frac{\xi^{-2+2\gamma_{0}}}{2\gamma_{0}-2}+\xi^{-2}-\frac{\xi^{-2-2\gamma_{0}}}{2\gamma_{0}+2}\right)

\displaystyle=\partial_{z}\left(\frac{\xi^{-1+2\gamma_{0}}}{(2\gamma_{0}-2)(2\gamma_{0}-1)}-\xi^{-1}+\frac{\xi^{-1-2\gamma_{0}}}{(2\gamma_{0}+2)(2\gamma_{0}+1)}\right)

\displaystyle=\partial_{z}\left(\frac{2\gamma_{0}\xi^{-1}}{(4\gamma_{0}^{2}-4)(4\gamma_{0}^{2}-1)}\left(2\gamma_{0}\left(\xi^{2\gamma_{0}}+\xi^{-2\gamma_{0}}\right)+3\left(\xi^{2\gamma_{0}}-\xi^{-2\gamma_{0}}\right)\right)\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\partial_{z}\left(\frac{\xi^{-1}}{(2\gamma_{0}^{2}-2)(4\gamma_{0}^{2}-1)}\left(\xi^{-2\gamma_{0}}\left(\xi^{2\gamma_{0}}-1\right)^{2}-2\gamma_{0}^{2}\left(4\gamma_{0}^{2}-4\right)\right)\right)

and also

\displaystyle 2k

\displaystyle\left(\frac{\xi^{-1+2\gamma_{0}}}{(2\gamma_{0}-2)(2\gamma_{0}-1)}-\xi^{-1}+\frac{\xi^{-1-2\gamma_{0}}}{(2\gamma_{0}+2)(2\gamma_{0}+1)}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{\partial_{z}\left((4\gamma_{0}^{2}+6\gamma_{0}+2)\frac{\xi^{2\gamma_{0}}-1}{2\gamma_{0}}-(4\gamma_{0}^{2}-4)(4\gamma_{0}^{2}-1)\log(\xi)+(4\gamma_{0}^{2}-6\gamma_{0}+2)\frac{1-\xi^{-2\gamma_{0}}}{2\gamma_{0}}\right)}{(4\gamma_{0}^{2}-4)(4\gamma_{0}^{2}-1)}

\displaystyle=\frac{\partial_{z}\left(2\gamma_{0}\left(\xi^{2\gamma_{0}}-\xi^{-2\gamma_{0}}\right)+3\left(\xi^{2\gamma_{0}}+\xi^{-2\gamma_{0}}-2\right)+2\mathrm{Q}_{\gamma_{0}}(\xi)\right)}{(4\gamma_{0}^{2}-4)(4\gamma_{0}^{2}-1)}

With the above formulae, the estimate follows by repeatedly integrating by parts the integral terms of the form
\mathrm{f}_{{\mathsf{s}},k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0},z)\partial_{z}^{n}h(z,y_{0})
for
n\geq 0
, we omit the routine details.
∎

Appendix G
Refined regularity of the spectral density function

Let
\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}
be the unique solution to (
12.1
). In this section we obtain improved bounds on

\displaystyle{\mathcal{V}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0}):=(v^{\prime}(y)-v^{\prime}(y_{0}))\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0}).

Firstly, we note that

\textsc{TG}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}{\mathcal{V}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})=v^{\prime\prime\prime}(z)\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}+2v^{\prime\prime}(z)\partial_{y}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(z,y_{0})+\frac{(v^{\prime}(y)-v^{\prime}(y_{0}))w_{k}^{0}(y)}{v(y)-v(y_{0})\pm i\varepsilon}+(v^{\prime}(y)-v^{\prime}(y_{0}))q_{k}^{0}(y)

(G.1)

together with
{\mathcal{V}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(0,y_{0})={\mathcal{V}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(2,y_{0})=0
.

Proposition G.1
.

We have

\displaystyle\|\partial_{y}^{n}{\mathcal{V}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\|_{H_{k}^{1}(I_{3}^{c}(y_{0}))}\lesssim k^{n-2}{\mathcal{S}}_{k,0},\quad\|\partial_{y}^{n}{\mathcal{V}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\|_{L^{\infty}(I_{3}^{c}(y_{0}))}\lesssim k^{n-\frac{3}{2}}{\mathcal{S}}_{k,0},

for
n=0,1
, uniformly for all
y_{0}\in(\vartheta_{1},\vartheta_{2})
.

Proof.

We first show the
H_{k}^{1}(I_{3}^{c}(y_{0}))
estimate for
n=0
. From (
G.1
) we can actually write

\displaystyle\textsc{RTG}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}{\mathcal{V}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})

\displaystyle=-\textsc{E}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}{\mathcal{V}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})+v^{\prime\prime\prime}(y)\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})+2v^{\prime\prime}(y)\partial_{y}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{(v^{\prime}(y)-v^{\prime}(y_{0}))w_{k}^{0}(y)}{v(y)-v(y_{0})\pm i\varepsilon}+(v^{\prime}(y)-v^{\prime}(y_{0}))q_{k}^{0}(y).

Appealing to the entanglement inequality Lemma
2.2
, we obtain

\displaystyle\|{\mathcal{V}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\|_{H_{k}^{1}(I_{3}^{c}(y_{0}))}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|{\mathcal{V}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\|_{L^{2}(I_{2}^{c}(y_{0})\cap I_{3}(y_{0}))}+\frac{1}{k^{2}}\|\textsc{E}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}{\mathcal{V}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\|_{L^{2}(I_{2}^{c}(y_{0}))}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{k^{2}}\|v^{\prime\prime\prime}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}+2v^{\prime\prime}\partial_{y}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\|_{L^{2}(I_{2}^{c}(y_{0}))}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{k^{2}}\left\|\frac{(v^{\prime}(y)-v^{\prime}(y_{0}))w_{k}^{0}}{v(y)-v(y_{0})\pm i\varepsilon}+(v^{\prime}(y)-v^{\prime}(y_{0}))q_{k}^{0}\right\|_{L^{2}(I_{2}^{c}(y_{0}))}.

Using Proposition
12.2
we easily get
\|{\mathcal{V}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\|_{L^{2}(I_{2}^{c}(y_{0})\cap I_{3}(y_{0}))}\lesssim k^{-2}{\mathcal{S}}_{k,0}
. On the other hand, we now have

\displaystyle\left|\textsc{E}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})(v^{\prime}(y)-v^{\prime}(y_{0}))\right|\lesssim 1

uniformly for all
y,y_{0}\in[0,2]
, so that Proposition
12.2
yields
\|\textsc{E}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}{\mathcal{V}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\|_{L^{2}(I_{2}^{c}(y_{0}))}\lesssim k^{-1}{\mathcal{S}}_{k,0}
. Similarly, we obtain
\|v^{\prime\prime\prime}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}+2v^{\prime\prime}\partial_{y}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\|_{L^{2}(I_{2}^{c}(y_{0}))}\lesssim{\mathcal{S}}_{k,0}
. Lastly,

\left\|\frac{(v^{\prime}(y)-v^{\prime}(y_{0}))w_{k}^{0}}{v(y)-v(y_{0})\pm i\varepsilon}+(v^{\prime}(y)-v^{\prime}(y_{0}))q_{k}^{0}\right\|_{L^{2}(I_{2}^{c}(y_{0}))}\lesssim{\mathcal{S}}_{k,0}.

Combining the estimates we obtain the desired
H_{k}^{1}(I_{3}^{c}(y_{0}))
bound for
{\mathcal{V}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}
. Those for
\partial_{y}{\mathcal{V}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}
follow then from (
G.1
). For the
L^{\infty}(I_{3}^{c}(y_{0}))
estimate on
\partial_{y}^{n}{\mathcal{V}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}
we proceed as in the proof of Proposition
12.2
, we omit the details.
∎

Proposition G.2
.

We have

\displaystyle\left\|\partial_{y_{0}}^{n}\left((v^{\prime}(z)-v^{\prime}(y_{0}))\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(z,y_{0})\right)\right\|_{L^{\infty}(y_{0}\in I_{3}^{c}(z))}\lesssim k^{-\frac{3}{2}}{\mathcal{S}}_{k,n}

for
n=0,1
.

Proof.

We argue as in the proof of Proposition
12.2
.

\diamond

Case
y\in I_{6}(y_{0})
.
Assume further that
y>y_{0}
. Then, for
y_{3}=y_{0}+\frac{3\beta}{k}
we have

\displaystyle\partial_{y_{0}}{\mathcal{V}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y,y_{0})=\partial_{y_{0}}{\mathcal{V}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y_{3},y_{0})+\int_{y_{3}}^{y}\partial_{y}\partial_{y_{0}}{\mathcal{V}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}.

Since
y_{3}\in I_{3}(y_{0})
, we have from Propositions
12.2
and
12.7
that

\displaystyle\left|\partial_{y_{0}}{\mathcal{V}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(y_{3},y_{0})\right|\lesssim k^{-\frac{3}{2}}{\mathcal{S}}_{k,1}.

On the other hand, since
y\in I_{6}(y_{0})
, there holds

\displaystyle\int_{y_{3}}^{y}\partial_{y}\partial_{y_{0}}{\mathcal{V}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(z,y_{0})\mathrm{d}z

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{y_{3}}^{y}\left(k^{-1}|\partial_{y,y_{0}}^{2}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(z,y_{0})|+|\partial_{y}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(z,y_{0})|+|\partial_{y}\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}(z,y_{0})|\right)\mathrm{d}z

\displaystyle\lesssim k^{-\frac{3}{2}}{\mathcal{S}}_{k,1}.

\diamond

Case
y\in I_{6}^{c}(y_{0})
.
Then
I_{3}(y)\cap I_{3}(y_{0})=\emptyset
, namely
I_{3}(y)\subset I_{3}^{c}(y_{0})
. Moreover, from Lemma
2.3
and Propositions
12.2
and
12.7
there holds

\displaystyle\|\partial_{y_{0}}{\mathcal{V}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\|_{L^{\infty}(I_{3}(y))}

\displaystyle\lesssim k^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{y_{0}}{\mathcal{V}}_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\|_{H_{k}^{1}(I_{3}(y))}

\displaystyle\lesssim k^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{y_{0}}(v^{\prime}(\cdot)-v^{\prime}(y_{0}))\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\|_{L^{2}(I_{3}(y))}

\displaystyle\quad+k^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{y}\partial_{y_{0}}(v^{\prime}(\cdot)-v^{\prime}(y_{0}))\varphi_{k,\varepsilon}^{\pm}\|_{L^{2}(I_{3}(y))}

\displaystyle\lesssim k^{-\frac{3}{2}}{\mathcal{S}}_{k,1}

since we used that
|v^{\prime}(z)-v^{\prime}(y_{0})|\lesssim k^{-1}
for
z\in I_{3}(y)
.

∎
